Method: 提出决策聚焦强化学习（DF-RL）框架，将预测器与RL充电策略进行端到端联合训练，使预测器接收来自RL智能体充电动作的反馈信号，从而优化最终决策质量而非单纯的预测精度。

Result: 相比不使用离开时间预测的RL方法，所提DF-RL方法实现了总奖励提升14%，未供应能量（因EV已离开而未能充电）减少55%。

Conclusion: 端到端的决策聚焦训练方法能有效缓解预测误差对下游控制策略的负面影响，显著提升电动汽车充电决策的质量。

Abstract: The recent growth of EV adoption poses challenges for power systems, including increased peak demand and potential grid instability. Smart control of EV charging -- e.g., based on reinforcement learning (RL) -- can alleviate these issues by learning temporal and contextual patterns from historical data. Yet, in real-world scenarios, key features, such as departure time, often are unavailable. This, in turn, makes it harder for an RL agent to learn and execute an effective charging policy. To mitigate this uncertainty, a trained forecaster can approximate the unknown features from available data. However, since these forecasting models are typically trained for accuracy (rather than their impact on a downstream agent's decision quality), their errors may propagate and hinder the overall performance of a controller that is using the forecasts. To avoid this, we propose a decision-focused RL (DF-RL) framework in which the forecaster is trained end-to-end, i.e., with feedback from the charging policy actions taken by the RL agent. Such joint training of both the forecaster and controller ultimately results in higher-quality actions: our proposed DF-RL method yields superior charging decisions compared to other baselines, achieving up to a 14% improvement in total reward and a 55% reduction of unsupplied energy (i.e., charging that failed to happen because the EV already left), relative to the RL method without departure time forecasting.

</details>


### [107] [Machine Unlearning for the XGBoost Model with Network Intrusion Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19220)
*Diana Magalhães,Eva Maia,João Vitorino,Isabel Praça*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了XGBoost-Forget，一种针对XGBoost模型的机器遗忘方法，用于解决网络入侵检测中表格数据的遗忘问题。在IoT-23和GeNIS数据集上的实验表明，该方法在保持预测性能的同时显著提升了遗忘效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘研究主要集中于深度学习和图像数据，忽视了网络入侵检测领域广泛使用的表格数据，存在研究空白。

Method: 提出XGBoost-Forget方法，针对XGBoost模型设计特定的遗忘机制，在两个表格网络入侵数据集(IoT-23和GeNIS)上进行评估。

Result: XGBoost-Forget在保持与原始模型相近的预测性能的同时，实现了显著更快的遗忘速度。

Conclusion: XGBoost-Forget在表格网络入侵检测场景中具有机器遗忘的潜力，为表格数据的遗忘研究提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Machine Unlearning (MU) has emerged as an important technique for removing specific data points from trained models without requiring full retraining. However, most existing MU research focuses on deep learning and image data, leaving a gap in the domain of network intrusion detection, which relies heavily on tabular data. This work introduces XGBoost-Forget, an unlearning approach for the XGBoost model, to address this gap. The approach is evaluated on two tabular Network Intrusion (NI) datasets, IoT-23 and GeNIS, using multiple metrics to assess model performance, unlearning efficiency, and forgetting quality. The results show that XGBoost-Forget maintains predictive performance close to the original model while providing significantly faster unlearning, demonstrating its potential for MU in tabular NI settings.

</details>


### [108] [Mechanism-Guided Selective Unlearning for RLVR-Induced Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19222)
*Chenyu Zhou,Qiliang Jiang,Shuning Wu,Xu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MAST是一种机制对齐的选择性遗忘方法，通过仅更新排名靠前的注意力投影张量子集，在遗忘RLVR引发的推理能力时显著降低附带损害，相比全参数更新能更好地保持模型在MATH和GSM8K等基准上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准全参数梯度上升在遗忘RLVR引入的推理能力时会造成严重的附带损害，破坏模型在保留基准（如MATH和GSM8K）上的性能。作者观察到SFT到RLVR的增量与SFT更新在token级delta-log-probability上存在显著差异，因此希望利用这一机制差异实现更有选择性的遗忘。

Method: MAST首先通过离主成分能量、更新幅度和遗忘梯度耦合幅度三个指标对注意力投影张量进行排序，然后仅更新排名靠前的子集，而非对所有参数进行梯度上升更新。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B上，MAST实现了统计显著的目标遗忘（MATH forget从45/150降至37/150，McNemar p=0.0078），同时保持了GSM8K（+0.8个百分点）和MATH retain（-0.5个百分点）性能。该优势在不同随机种子、NPO/SimNPO损失函数以及Qwen3模型上均得到复现，全参数更新在Qwen3上导致GSM8K性能崩溃，而MAST则保持了该性能。

Conclusion: MAST通过机制对齐的参数选择策略，在遗忘RLVR引起的推理能力时能够有效降低附带损害，是一种比标准全参数更新更优的模型遗忘方法。

Abstract: We propose MAST (Mechanism-Aligned Selective Targeting), a mechanism-guided method for unlearning RLVR-induced reasoning with substantially lower collateral damage than standard full-parameter updates. In matched SFT/RLVR checkpoints on Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B and Qwen3-1.7B-Base, the SFT-to-RLVR increment differs sharply from the SFT update in token-level delta-log-probability, and full-parameter gradient ascent forgets only by damaging retain MATH and GSM8K. MAST ranks attention-projection tensors by off-principal energy, update magnitude, and forget-gradient coupling magnitude, then updates only the top-ranked subset. On the primary model, MAST induces statistically significant target forgetting (MATH forget 45/150 to 37/150; McNemar p=0.0078) while preserving GSM8K (+0.8 pp) and MATH retain (-0.5 pp). The advantage reproduces across seeds, NPO/SimNPO objectives, and Qwen3, where MAST preserves GSM8K while full-parameter unlearning collapses it.

</details>


### [109] [A Human-in-the-Loop Bayesian Optimization Framework for Constraint-Aware Bioprocess Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19230)
*Samuel Stricker,Claus Wirnsperger,Alessandro Butté,Laura Helleckes,Gonzalo Guillén Gosálbez,Antonio del Rio Chanona,Mehmet Mercangöz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Pareto前沿引导采样(PFGS)框架，将约束优化(通过GP后验概率显式建模规格满足概率)和鲁棒优化(通过蒙特卡洛采样估计输入扰动下的期望下限性能)整合到人机协同贝叶斯优化中，通过交互式多目标Pareto前沿可视化实现专家驱动的高效候选选择，并在八维CHO细胞培养模拟中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有贝叶斯优化方法通常返回单一自动化推荐，缺乏领域专家在优化过程中的交互参与，且难以同时处理性能预测、模型不确定性、约束满足和输入鲁棒性等多维权衡。本研究旨在扩展PFGS框架，使其能够支持约束优化和鲁棒优化，并通过交互式可视化让专家在迭代过程中根据多维Pareto前沿做出明智决策。

Method: 1) 约束优化：将满足输出规格限的后验概率作为显式Pareto目标，利用GP后验分布解析计算；2) 鲁棒优化：采用蒙特卡洛采样策略，在用户定义输入扰动分布上估计期望下限性能；3) 通过交互式仪表盘展示成对二维Pareto前沿投影，使性能、不确定性、约束概率和鲁棒性等多维权衡可视化。

Result: 在八维补料分批CHO细胞培养模拟器上，该框架系统性地识别出高性能、满足可行性约束且对扰动具有鲁棒性的操作条件，证明了专家定义的需求可作为原则性停止准则，并支持实验资源的合理分配。

Conclusion: 该扩展框架将人机协同贝叶斯优化的能力从单一目标性能优化拓展到同时处理约束满足和输入鲁棒性，通过交互式多目标可视化使专家能够在模型改进和目标演化过程中迭代优化选择标准，为复杂工业过程优化提供了实用的决策支持工具。

Abstract: This work presents an extension to Pareto Front Guided Sampling (PFGS), a Human-in-the-Loop (HitL) Bayesian Optimization (BO) framework in which Gaussian process (GP) surrogate-derived quantities are reformulated as objectives of a multi-objective optimization problem, and the resulting Pareto front is exposed to a domain expert for interactive candidate selection rather than returning a single automated recommendation. The framework is extended in two directions: constrained optimization is addressed by incorporating the posterior probability of satisfying output specification limits as an explicit Pareto objective, computed analytically from the GP posterior distribution; robust optimization is addressed by a Monte Carlo sampling strategy that estimates expected lower-confidence performance over a user-defined variability of input perturbations, capturing performance degradation under likely implementation deviations. The resulting multi-dimensional Pareto representation renders trade-offs between predicted performance, model uncertainty, probabilistic constraint satisfaction, and input robustness simultaneously visible through pairwise two-dimensional projections on an interactive dashboard, enabling selection criteria to be iteratively refined as the surrogate model improves and development objectives evolve. The framework is showcased on an eight-dimensional fed-batch Chinese Hamster Ovary (CHO) cell culture simulator demonstrating systematic identification of high-performing, feasibility-compliant, and perturbation-resilient operating conditions, and illustrating how expert-defined requirements provide a principled stopping criterion and support informed allocation of experimental resources.

</details>


### [110] [STARE: Surprisal-Guided Token-Level Advantage Reweighting for Policy Entropy Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19236)
*Haipeng Luo,Qingfeng Sun,Songli Wu,Can Xu,Wenfeng Deng,Han Hu,Yansong Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过梯度分析发现GRPO算法存在token级信用分配不匹配问题，提出STARE方法，通过surprisal引导的token级优势重加权和目标熵闭环控制，在1.5B-32B规模模型上实现稳定熵调控，在AIME24/25上超越DAPO等基线4%-8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO等RLVR算法在复杂推理训练中普遍遭遇策略熵坍塌问题，导致探索-利用失衡，限制了RL训练潜力。

Method: 1) 对GRPO下token级熵动态进行一阶梯度分析，揭示熵变化分解为轨迹级优势与next-token分布熵灵敏度函数的乘积，形成优势-惊异四象限结构和近临界性；2) 提出STARE：通过batch内surprisal分位数识别熵关键token子集，选择性重加权其有效优势，并引入目标熵闭环门控实现稳定熵调控。

Result: 在1.5B-32B规模、Short CoT/Long CoT/Multi-Turn Tool Use三个任务族上，STARE数千步训练保持熵在目标带内；AIME24/25上比DAPO等基线平均准确率提升4%-8%，反思token和响应长度同步增长，表明探索-利用平衡持续改善。

Conclusion: STARE有效解决了RLVR训练中的熵坍塌问题，通过细粒度token级信用分配校正实现了更稳定的训练和更强的推理性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards algorithms like GRPO have emerged as the dominant post-training paradigm for complex reasoning in LLMs, yet commonly suffer from policy entropy collapse during training. We conduct a first-order gradient analysis of token-level entropy dynamics under GRPO and identify a token-level credit assignment mismatch: the per-token entropy variation decomposes into the product of the trajectory-level advantage and an entropy sensitivity function over the next-token distribution, yielding an advantage-surprisal four-quadrant structure and a near-criticality property. Motivated by it, we propose STARE (Surprisal-guided Token-level Advantage Reweighting for policy Entropy stability), which identifies entropy-critical token subsets via batch-internal surprisal quantiles, selectively reweights their effective advantages, and incorporates a target-entropy closed-loop gate for stable entropy regulation. Across model scales from 1.5B to 32B and three task families (Short CoT, Long CoT, and Multi-Turn Tool Use), STARE sustains stable RL training over thousands of steps while maintaining policy entropy within the target band. On AIME24 and AIME25, STARE outperforms DAPO and other competitive baselines by 4%-8% in average accuracy, with reflection tokens and response length growing in tandem, indicating sustained exploration-exploitation balance that further unlocks RL training potential.Code is available at https://github.com/hp-luo/STARE.

</details>


### [111] [SCAN: Enhance Time Series Anomaly Detection via Multi-Scale Neighborhood-Centered Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19255)
*Xingze Zheng,Hanyin Cheng,Siyuan Wang,Yiting Hao,Peng Chen,Yuan Jun,Yang Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SCAN方法，通过多尺度聚类增强基于重建的时间序列异常检测，在表示层和异常准则层分别引入聚类中心约束和聚类隶属度置信分数，有效解决了重建方法的过泛化与欠泛化问题，在多领域真实数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于重建的时间序列异常检测方法存在过泛化（模型也能重建异常）和欠泛化（无法充分重建正常模式）的难题，两者难以平衡，亟需一种能够约束模型专注于代表性正常模式的检测方法。

Method: 1）在表示层引入正常模式的聚类中心表示，约束模型针对代表性正常模式进行重建，防止模型强大容量导致的过泛化；2）在异常准则层，基于聚类隶属概率推导异常置信分数，与重建误差构成双准则检测；3）提取邻域中心表示进行多视图聚类以提升聚类性能。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集（涵盖不同应用领域）上的大量实验表明，SCAN方法达到了最先进的性能水平。

Conclusion: 多尺度聚类能够有效增强重建式时间序列异常检测，通过双层次约束（表示层+异常准则层）平衡过泛化与欠泛化问题，且聚类质量的提升（通过邻域中心多视图聚类）进一步保障了方法效果。

Abstract: Time series anomaly detection plays a crucial role in a wide range of real-world applications. Reconstruction-based methods have become the mainstream paradigm, but they suffer from over-generalization and under-generalization problems, which are challenging to balance. To address this, we introduce multi-scale clustering to enhance reconstruction-based methods. At the representation level, we integrate the cluster center representations of normal patterns to constrain the model to target representative normal patterns for reconstruction, preventing dominance of powerful capacity and representation capability. At the anomaly criterion level, we derive anomaly confidence score based on cluster membership probability and combine it with reconstruction error, providing dual criteria for detection. Furthermore, the effectiveness of the cluster center representations and anomaly confidence score depends on the clustering performance. Accordingly, we extract neighborhood-centered representations for multi-view clustering to improve clustering performance. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world datasets from diverse application domains demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance of SCAN.

</details>


### [112] [Detecting Hidden ML Training With Zero-Overhead Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19262)
*Robi Rahman,Sabiha Tajdari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过5轮攻防迭代，评估了基于NVML遥测的GPU工作负载分类在对抗环境下的鲁棒性，发现分类器在训练负载识别上达到98.2%准确率，但面对精心伪装的最具挑战性负载时准确率降至43-87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件监控是实现AI计算治理（如确保算力不被滥用）的关键技术路径，但如果攻击者可以轻易逃避监控机制，相关方案便不可行。因此需要系统评估GPU工作负载分类在对抗环境下的鲁棒性。

Method: 使用零开销、隐私保护的NVML遥测数据（不访问模型权重、训练数据或超参数），在5轮监控-逃避迭代中评估20种逃避策略家族，覆盖4代架构共9种GPU型号，构建分类器进行对抗鲁棒性测试。

Result: 分类器在识别训练负载时达到98.2%的二分类准确率，但面对最具挑战性的非预期负载时，即使经过对抗伪装，准确率也降至43-87%。

Conclusion: 基于零开销NVML遥测的GPU工作负载分类在应对一般攻击时鲁棒性较强，但面对精心设计的对抗伪装，准确率显著下降，表明当前方案尚需进一步加固才能支撑可靠的AI计算治理。

Abstract: Hardware-enabled monitoring of GPU workloads underpins many proposals for AI compute governance, but if developers can defeat monitoring mechanisms, such schemes are unworkable. We evaluate the adversarial robustness of GPU workload classification using only zero-overhead, privacy-preserving NVML telemetry: content-agnostic signals that observe physical effects of computation without accessing model weights, training data, or hyperparameters. Across 5 rounds of monitor-evader iteration, we evaluate 20 evasion strategy families on 9 GPU models spanning 4 architecture generations. We develop a classifier that achieves 98.2% binary accuracy at identifying training workloads across the whole corpus, and 43-87% accuracy against the most challenging unexpected workloads even when they are adversarially disguised.

</details>


### [113] [Structured Inference with Large Language Gibbs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19264)
*Sanghyeok Choi,Henry Gouk,Esmeralda S. Whitammer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Large Language Gibbs方法，利用LLM的条件分布作为马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）中的转移算子，通过迭代重采样单个变量实现结构化概率推理，避免单次自回归生成中的顺序偏差，并在合成分布、一致性推理和贝叶斯结构学习任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码的知识可用于复杂世界的结构化推理，但如何以概率一致的方式访问这些知识是一个困难的推理问题。现有单次自回归生成存在顺序偏差，无法保证概率一致性。

Method: 提出Large Language Gibbs（LLG）方案，将LLM的next-token条件分布作为MCMC中的转移算子，在吉布斯采样框架下迭代地重采样每个变量，条件于其他变量的当前值，从而获得平稳分布。

Result: 在合成分布采样、一致性推理任务和贝叶斯结构学习三个场景中验证了方法有效性，表明LLM条件分布结合MCMC是单次自回归生成的有实际意义的替代方案。

Conclusion: 利用LLM条件分布进行MCMC采样是在有噪声的LLM世界先验下进行结构化概率推理的实用方案，能避免顺序偏差并产生平衡所有局部条件分布的平稳分布。

Abstract: The knowledge encoded in large language models (LLMs) can serve as a substrate for structured reasoning over variables describing a complex world, but accessing this knowledge in a probabilistically coherent manner poses a difficult inference problem. We propose Large Language Gibbs, a scheme for structured probabilistic inference that uses conditional distributions of an LLM as transition operators. Rather than sampling structured objects through single-pass autoregressive generation, we iteratively resample individual variables conditioned on others using an LLM's next-token conditionals. This approach avoids order-dependent biases and produces a stationary distribution that reflects a compromise between all local conditionals. We apply this approach to sampling from synthetic distributions, consistent reasoning tasks, and Bayesian structure learning. The results suggest that the use of LLM conditionals in MCMC is a practical alternative to one-pass generation for structured probabilistic inference under a world prior accessible through noisy LLM conditionals.

</details>


### [114] [Risk Stratification for ICU Delirium using Pervasive Ambient Sensing Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19292)
*Jiaqing Zhang,Sabyasachi Bandyopadhyay,Miguel Contreras,Jessica Sena,Yuanfang Ren,Andrea Davidson,Ziyuan Guan,Tezcan Ozrazgat-Baslanti,Subhash Nerella,Azra Bihorac,Parisa Rashidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了环境因素（光强度和声压级）对ICU患者谵妄的预测能力，使用四种神经网络模型在9个ICU的309名患者数据上进行评估。卷积模型在声音数据上表现最佳（AUC=0.80），声音特征是主要预测因子，结合光数据可改善短期（<1周）预测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谵妄是ICU常见且严重的并发症，现有风险评估常忽略环境因素（如光照和声音）。本研究旨在探究环境光声数据能否独立预测谵妄，以补充现有预测模型，改善ICU谵妄的预防策略。

Method: 收集9个ICU共309名患者的环境光强和声压级数据，采用四种高效的时序神经网络模型，在10种不同预测窗口大小下进行谵妄预测，并使用Shapley Additive Explanations分析特征重要性与影响方向。

Result: 卷积模型在声音数据上AUC=0.80，与组合数据表现相当；声音特征是整体主导预测因子；结合光数据可改善短期（<1周）预测；组合模型在感知期后立即分配最高风险。

Conclusion: 被动环境感知（尤其是声音）可为谵妄风险评估提供临床上有意义的可解释信号，为丰富多模态ICU预测和预防策略提供实用路径。

Abstract: Delirium is a common and serious complication in the Intensive Care Unit (ICU), associated with increased morbidity, prolonged hospital stays, and higher healthcare costs. Despite its prevalence, early prediction and prevention remain challenging. Environmental factors such as ambient sound and light may influence the onset of delirium, yet they are often overlooked in risk assessments. In this study, we examined whether light intensity and sound pressure levels can independently predict delirium across multiple prediction horizons. We evaluated four efficient sequential neural network models on data collected from 9 ICUs across 309 patients to predict delirium for 10 prediction-window sizes. We reported feature importance and direction of influence using Shapley Additive Explanations analysis. The convolutional model achieved the strongest discrimination, with AUC = 0.80 on sound data and on combined data. Sound features were the dominant predictors overall. Integrating sound with light improved short-term ($<1$ week) prediction, with the combined model assigning the highest risk immediately after the sensing period. These findings suggest that passive ambient sensing, especially sound, can add a clinically meaningful, interpretable signal for delirium risk estimation and offer a practical pathway to enrich multimodal ICU prediction and prevention strategies.

</details>


### [115] [Does VLA Even Know the Basics? Measuring Commonsense and World Knowledge Retention in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19297)
*Nikita Kachaev,Andrey Moskalenko,Matvey Skripkin,Nikita Kurlaev,Daria Pugacheva,Albina Burlova,Mikhail Kolosov,Denis Shepelev,Andrey Kuznetsov,Elena Tutubalina,Aleksandr I. Panov,Alexey K. Kovalev,Vlad Shakhuro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Act2Answer协议，通过将VLM知识基准转化为机器人动作选择任务，评估VLA模型在微调后保留常识和事实知识的能力。在7个VLA模型和9个VLM基线的系统研究中发现：VLA在简单概念上表现良好，但在丰富语义类别上相比源VLM存在较大差距；VQA协同训练有助于知识保留；答案相关信号在VLA中间层达到峰值但在上层衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型通常从预训练的VLM微调而来，但微调后模型保留了多少常识和事实知识尚不明确。知识敏感任务的失败难以区分是知识缺失还是低层控制泛化不足导致。

Method: 提出Act2Answer协议：将VLM知识基准适配到VLA评估，每个问题转化为一个简短桌面操作场景，智能体通过单个物体放置动作选择候选答案，得到基于动作的成功率；同时引入逐层意图探针来定位VLM骨干和动作头中与答案相关的信息。

Result: VLA在简单概念上表现良好，但在丰富语义类别上与源VLM差距较大；VQA协同训练有助于知识保留；答案相关信号在VLA中间层达到峰值但在上层衰减。

Conclusion: Act2Answer能够有效评估VLA模型的知识保留能力，揭示了VLA微调过程中的知识衰减现象，并指出VQA协同训练和中间层特征利用对缓解该问题的潜在价值。

Abstract: Embodied Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are typically obtained by fine-tuning powerful pretrained VLMs on robotics data, yet it is unclear how much commonsense and factual knowledge they retain after adaptation. Failures on knowledge-sensitive tasks are ambiguous, conflating missing knowledge with poor generalization of low-level control. We introduce Act2Answer, a lightweight protocol that adapts VLM knowledge benchmarks to VLA evaluation by requiring agents to answer through action. Each question becomes a short tabletop episode where the agent performs a single object-placement action to select among candidate answers, yielding an action-grounded success rate with reduced control confounds. We curate a test suite of such environments across diverse commonsense and world-knowledge categories and introduce layerwise intent probing to localize answer-relevant information across the VLM backbone and action head. In a large-scale study of 7 VLA models and 9 VLM baselines, we systematically rank models across categories, finding that VLAs show solid performance on simple concepts while exhibiting larger gaps on richer semantic categories relative to their source VLMs, that VQA co-training is associated with better knowledge retention, and that answer-relevant signals peak in middle VLA layers but attenuate in upper layers. Act2Answer is available at https://tttonyalpha.github.io/act2answer/.

</details>


### [116] [P-K-GCN: Physics-augmented Koopman-enhanced Graph Convolutional Network for Deep Spatiotemporal Super-resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19303)
*Xizhuo,Zhang,Zekai Wang,Fei Liu,Bing Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理增强的Koopman图卷积网络(P-K-GCN)，用于不规则几何上的时空超分辨率重建。该方法结合样条基图卷积和Koopman算子理论提取时空特征，并通过物理约束损失提升预测保真度。理论分析证明该方法能降低Rademacher复杂度、收紧泛化界。在3D心脏电动力学重建任务上取得了优于基线模型的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真时空动力学模拟计算成本极高，需要高效超分辨率技术从粗粒度输入重建高分辨率数据。传统数据驱动方法缺乏物理约束，简单物理信息学习难以处理不规则空间几何和复杂演化的时间动力学。

Method: 1) 设计连续样条基图卷积网络(GCN)从粗粒度图中提取空间依赖关系；2) 引入Koopman算子理论将非线性动力学投影到紧致潜空间，实现时间演化的线性化；3) 增加基于物理的损失项，强制数据驱动重建遵守物理定律；4) 提供理论分析证明物理增强和Koopman正则化可降低Rademacher复杂度并收紧泛化界。

Result: 在3D心脏几何上从稀疏低分辨率测量重建高分辨率心脏电动力学，数值实验表明该方法在精度上优于基线模型。

Conclusion: 物理增强和Koopman正则化在数学上保证了超分辨率误差的降低，所提方法在不规则几何上能够实现高精度的时空超分辨率重建。

Abstract: High-fidelity simulation of spatiotemporal dynamics is computationally prohibitive, necessitating efficient super-resolution techniques to reconstruct high-resolution data from coarse-grained inputs. Traditional data-driven methods often lack physical constraints, and simple physics-informed learning struggles with irregular spatial geometries and intricately evolving temporal dynamics. To tackle these challenges, we propose a Physics-augmented Koopman-enhanced Graph Convolutional Network (P-K-GCN) for spatiotemporal super-resolution on irregular geometries. Specifically, a continuous spline-based GCN is first designed to extract spatial dependencies directly from coarse graph, and Koopman operator theory is incorporated to project the nonlinear dynamics into a compact latent space where temporal progression is linearized. Second, we augment the optimization objective with a physics-based loss to force the data-driven reconstructions to adhere to physical laws for improving predictive fidelity and robustness. Finally, we provide a rigorous theoretical analysis, establishing that the physics augmentation and Koopman regularization mathematically guarantees a reduction in super-resolution error by diminishing Rademacher complexity and tightening generalization bounds. We evaluate our framework on reconstructing spatially high-resolution cardiac electrodynamics across a 3D heart geometry from sparse low-resolution measurements. Numerical experiments demonstrate that our method achieves superior accuracy compared to baseline models.

</details>


### [117] [Diffusion-Proof: Recipe for Formal Theorem Proving Beyond Auto-Regressive Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19315)
*Ruida Wang,Rui Pan,Pengcheng Wang,Shizhe Diao,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Diffusion-Proof，首个将扩散大语言模型（dLLM）应用于形式化定理证明的框架，包含用于整体证明生成的 dLLM-Prover-7B 和用于局部校正的 dLLM-Corrector-7B，在 ProofNet-Test 和 MiniF2F-Test 上分别相对提升 1.61% 和 6.14%，并成功解决了一个 DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B 未能解决的 IMO 问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归LLM在形式化定理证明中因长程连贯性不足和错误累积导致次优性能，而扩散LLM在多token块迭代去噪方面具有优势，但其在形式化数学中的应用尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出 Diffusion-Proof 框架，包含两个模型：(1) dLLM-Prover-7B，利用扩散LLM进行整条证明的生成，保持长程连贯的策略使用；(2) dLLM-Corrector-7B，利用扩散LLM的填充能力，基于双向信息进行局部证明校正。

Result: 在 ProofNet-Test 上绝对提升 1.61%，在 MiniF2F-Test 上绝对提升 6.14%，相对显著优于同数据集训练的AR LLM基线；成功解决一个DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B未能解决的IMO问题。

Conclusion: 扩散LLM在形式化定理证明中展现出独特优势，尤其在需要长程连贯性和局部校正的场景下，为形式化数学推理提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Enhancing the formal math reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) has become a key focus in both mathematical and computer science communities in recent years. While significant progress has been made in using state-of-the-art Auto-Regressive (AR) LLMs for formal theorem proving, these models suffer from inherent limitations. Their next-token prediction generation methods may yield suboptimal performance due to the challenges of long-range coherence and the compounding of errors over long sequences. Recent advancements in diffusion LLMs (dLLMs), which generate text through iterative denoising of a multi-token block, offer a promising alternative. However, the application of dLLMs to formal mathematics, where maintaining long-range coherence is critical, remains largely understudied. To address the challenges above, we propose **Diffusion-Proof**, to the best of our knowledge, the first framework to train and apply dLLMs for formal theorem proving. Our frameworks contain training and inference methods for two models. The first one is *dLLM-Prover-7B*, which performs whole-proof writing with long-range coherent tactic usage. The second one is *dLLM-Corrector-7B*, which is a novel large block diffusion-based correction model. It leverages the in-filling capabilities of dLLMs to perform local proof correction using bi-directional information. Extensive experiments demonstrate that **Diffusion-Proof** relatively significantly outperforms the AR LLM baseline trained under the same dataset. **Diffusion-Proof** achieves an absolute improvement of **1.61%** on ProofNet-Test and **6.14%** on MiniF2F-Test benchmarks compare to the baseline. Notably, **Diffusion-Proof** successfully resolves one IMO problem that more advanced thinking model DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B could not solve, showcasing the unique advantage of dLLMs in formal theorem proving.

</details>


### [118] [Explaining Attention with Program Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19317)
*Amiri Hayes,Belinda Li,Jacob Andreas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可扩展的方法，通过提示预训练语言模型生成Python程序来近似替代Transformer语言模型中的注意力头，实现了用可执行、人类可读的符号化描述替代神经计算的目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释深度学习的长远目标是使用人类可理解的符号化描述替代不透明的神经计算，但现有方法在可扩展性和语义保真度方面存在不足，需要一种能够自动将神经网络组件行为翻译为可执行程序的方法。

Method: 首先对给定注意力头在随机训练样本上计算注意力矩阵；然后以这些矩阵的摘要提示预训练语言模型，生成能根据输入文本复现注意力模式的Python程序；最后基于在保留输入上的预测表现对程序进行重排序筛选。

Result: 在GPT-2、TinyLlama-1.1B和Llama-3B上，不到1000个生成程序即可复现注意力模式，在TinyStories上平均IoU相似度超过75%；替换25%注意力头仅造成平均16%的困惑度增加，且在下游问答基准上保持性能。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个可扩展的逆向工程注意力头的流水线，使用人类可读的可执行代码替代神经组件，推动了神经网络符号化透明性的发展。

Abstract: A longstanding goal of research on interpretable deep learning is to replace opaque neural computations with human-meaningful symbolic descriptions. In this paper, we propose an approach for approximating the behavior of components of deep networks with executable programs. We focus on attention heads in transformer language models. For a given head, we first compute its associated attention matrices on a collection of randomly selected training examples. Next, we prompt a pre-trained language model with a summary of these matrices, and instruct it to generate a set of Python programs that can reproduce the associated attention patterns given only text from the input sentence. Finally, we re-rank programs according to how well our final set of programs predict behavior on held-out inputs. We demonstrate that a set of fewer than 1,000 such generated programs can reproduce the attention patterns of heads in GPT-2, TinyLlama-1.1B, and Llama-3B, achieving an average Intersection-over-Union similarity above 75% on TinyStories. Moreover, the best-fit programs can replace neural attention heads without substantially affecting model behavior: replacing 25% of attention heads with programmatic surrogates across the three models incurs only a 16% average perplexity increase, while maintaining performance on a variety of downstream question answering benchmarks. This work contributes a scalable pipeline for reverse-engineering attention heads in transformer models using human-readable, executable code, advancing a path toward symbolic transparency in neural models.

</details>


### [119] [UBP2: Uncertainty-Balanced Preference Planning for Efficient Preference-based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19328)
*Mohamed Nabail,Leo Cheng,Jingmin Wang,Nicholas Rhinehart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于模型的不确定性平衡偏好规划方法（UBP2），通过联合推理奖励、动力学和值函数的不确定性，在偏好学习框架下实现主动探索，显著提升样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于偏好的强化学习方法依赖被动数据收集，样本效率低下，尤其在学习的早期阶段表现不佳。

Method: UBP2采用集成模型（奖励、动力学、值函数）评估候选轨迹，根据联合评分（期望奖励+终值+认知不确定性）进行规划，实现探索与利用的显式权衡。

Result: 在标准正则性假设下，建立了有限时域和无限时域下的次线性遗憾界；在Meta-World基准上，UBP2的样本效率显著优于无模型偏好方法和非乐观的基于模型基线。

Conclusion: UBP2通过主动不确定性引导的探索，有效解决了偏好学习中样本效率低下的问题，无需启发式探索策略。

Abstract: Preference-based RL provides an approach to learning reward models from pairwise comparisons of behaviors, bypassing the need for explicit reward design. However, existing methods typically rely on passive data collection and suffer from poor sample efficiency, especially during the early stages of learning. We introduce a model-based approach that actively directs exploration by jointly reasoning over uncertainties in the reward, dynamics, and value functions. Our method, Uncertainty-Balanced Preference Planning (UBP2), uses ensembles of reward, dynamics, and value function models to evaluate candidate trajectories according to a unified score that combines expected reward, terminal value, and epistemic uncertainty. Planning under this objective yields an explicit tradeoff between exploitation and information acquisition without requiring ad hoc exploration heuristics. Under standard regularity assumptions, we establish sublinear regret guarantees for both finite-horizon and infinite-horizon settings. Empirically, experiments on the Meta-World benchmark show UBP2 achieves substantially higher sample efficiency than model-free preference-based methods and non-optimistic model-based baselines.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [Electromagnetic Characterization of Magnetic Ring: Case of Circular Cross-Section Shape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18270)
*Taha El Hajji,Lars Sjöberg*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了环形磁芯（圆形截面）在正弦激励下的二维解析模型，基于Maxwell方程和复磁导率，推导了内部磁场、磁通、复阻抗和总损耗的解析表达式，并严格分离了涡流损耗、磁滞损耗和绕组损耗，通过Bessel函数显式考虑集肤效应，为标准化磁性材料表征提供了高效准确的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有磁性材料表征依赖2D/3D有限元分析，计算成本高；而传统解析模型缺乏对环形磁芯圆形截面的完整二维分析，无法准确分离各类损耗并考虑集肤效应。

Method: 在局部极坐标系中应用Maxwell方程，结合复磁导率框架；利用Bessel函数解析描述集肤效应；推导磁场、磁通、复阻抗和损耗的封闭解析表达式；引入表观磁导率将非线性磁芯行为映射到简化线性模型。

Result: 成功推导了环形磁芯内部磁场、磁通、复阻抗和总损耗的完整解析表达式；实现了涡流损耗、磁滞损耗和绕组损耗的严格分离；表观磁导率表达式可用于简化非线性建模。

Conclusion: 该解析模型计算高效且精确，可作为Brockhaus和Iwatsu环测量等标准化表征的可靠工具，是2D/3D有限元分析的有力替代方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces a comprehensive two-dimensional analytical model of a toroidal magnetic ring with circular cross-section under sinusoidal excitation. Applying Maxwell's equations in local polar coordinates within a complex permeability, the model derives analytical expressions for the internal magnetic field, magnetic flux, complex impedance, and total losses. It rigorously separates the contributions of eddy current losses, hysteresis losses, and winding losses, while explicitly incorporating the skin effect in the conductive core via Bessel functions. An expression for the apparent permeability is also provided, enabling the nonlinear core behavior to be mapped onto simplified linear material models. The resulting analytical model offers a computationally efficient and accurate foundation for standardized magnetic material characterization, such as Brockhaus and Iwatsu ring measurements, as a powerful alternative to 2D and 3D finite element analysis.

</details>


### [121] [Deep Research in Physical Sciences: A Multi-Agent Framework and Comprehensive Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18648)
*Yigeng Jiang,Tengchao Yang,Taoyong Cui,Jiaxing Wan,Yuan Wang,Weida Wang,Zhiyu Liu,Chuyi Peng,Binzhao Luo,Maoli Gao,Huaihai Huang,Yuqianer Zeng,Ziyang Zheng,Dongchen Huang,Chao Chen,Zichao Liu,Weiping Shen,Shuchen Pu,Siyu Zhou,Runmin Ma,Yusong Hu,Fei Chao,Bo Zhang,Xiawu Zheng,Zifu Wang,Lei Bai,Yunqi Cai,Shufei Zhang*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了面向物理科学研究的基准PhySciBench（包含200道专家筛选的物理/化学问题），发现当前最强模型Gemini Deep Research准确率仅33.5%，并识别出三大缺陷：长推理链脆弱性、跨步骤知识迁移有限、缺乏物理 grounding 的自我验证。基于此开发了模块化多智能体框架DelveAgent，配备自适应规划循环、双粒度记忆和分层物理接地反思机制，在四个科学基准上提升准确率最高7.5个百分点，并将推理成本降至最强基线的约三分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对LLM-based深度研究智能体在物理科学领域能力的全面深入评估，需要建立专门的高质量基准来系统评测其局限性，并据此设计更可靠的架构。

Method: 1) 构建PhySciBench基准：200道专家精选题目，均衡覆盖物理和化学，分6类任务反映真实科研工作流；2) 评估现有SOTA模型和智能体系统；3) 分析失败案例归纳三大缺陷；4) 开发DelveAgent多智能体框架：模块化设计，包含自适应规划循环、双粒度记忆、分层物理接地反思机制。

Result: PhySciBench上最强基线Gemini Deep Research准确率仅33.5%；DelveAgent在四个科学基准上准确率提升最高7.5个百分点，推理成本降至最强基线的约1/3。

Conclusion: PhySciBench是评估物理科学领域AI系统的重要基准；架构专业化（多智能体、自适应规划、物理接地反思）能有效增强自主科学研究的可靠性。

Abstract: Deep research agents are Large Language Model (LLM)-based systems designed for autonomous, multi-step scientific reasoning, and they hold immense potential for accelerating research in the physical sciences. However, comprehensive and in-depth evaluations of their capabilities within this domain remain lacking. To address this gap, we introduce PhySciBench, a benchmark highly relevant to physical science research, comprising 200 expert-curated questions, balanced between physics and chemistry, across six task categories that reflect real-world scientific workflows. Evaluations of state-of-the-art models and agent systems on PhySciBench reveal limited performance; even the strongest baseline, Gemini Deep Research, achieves an accuracy of only 33.5%. Analysis of failure cases identifies three recurrent deficiencies: fragility in extended reasoning chains, limited knowledge transfer across steps, and a lack of physics-grounded self-verification. Motivated by these findings, we develop DelveAgent, a modular multi-agent framework equipped with an adaptive planning loop, dual-granularity memory, and a hierarchical physics-grounded reflection mechanism. Across four scientific benchmarks, DelveAgent improves accuracy by up to 7.5 percentage points while reducing inference costs to approximately one-third of the strongest baseline. These results establish the significance of PhySciBench as a critical benchmark for evaluating AI systems in the physical sciences and demonstrate that architectural specialization can effectively enhance the reliability of autonomous scientific research.

</details>


### [122] [Extension of a multi-region free-surface MHD solver beyond the inductionless approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18745)
*Min Ki Jung,Brian Wynne,Francisco Saenz,Yufan Xu,Jabir Al-Salami,Yong-Su Na,Egemen Kolemen*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出FreeMHD2，将开源求解器FreeMHD从无感应近似扩展到自洽求解感应磁场，采用矢量势形式自动满足$\nabla\cdot\boldsymbol{B}=0$，并在多区域两相流框架下通过解析解和LMX-U实验验证，成为首个无需无感应近似、经实验验证的开源自由表面液态金属求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自由表面液态金属MHD求解器依赖无感应近似（忽略感应磁场），无法处理有限磁雷诺数条件下的瞬态聚变事件。需要开发能够自洽求解感应磁场的求解器。

Method: 在开源求解器FreeMHD基础上，采用矢量势（vector-potential）公式自动满足$\nabla\cdot\boldsymbol{B}=0$约束，突破无感应近似限制，保留原有的多区域、两相框架。

Result: 通过Shercliff和Hunt管道流动解析解（多个Hartmann数范围）进行验证，并通过LMX-U实验的自由表面高度测量数据进行实验验证。FreeMHD2成为首个开源、经实验验证、无需无感应近似的自由表面液态金属求解器。

Conclusion: 去除而非放松无感应近似，为未来大规模瞬态聚变事件中有限磁雷诺数条件的数值模拟提供了基础。

Abstract: Free-surface liquid metal flows are a leading candidate for the plasma-facing components of future fusion reactors. Existing transient, three-dimensional, free-surface MHD solvers rely on the inductionless approximation in which the induced magnetic field is neglected. This paper extends the open-source solver FreeMHD [B. Wynne et al., Phys. Plasmas 32, 013907 (2025)] beyond the inductionless approximation to resolve the induced magnetic field self-consistently using a vector-potential formulation that enforces $\nabla\cdot\boldsymbol{B}=0$ by construction while preserving the original multi-region, two-phase framework. The solver is verified against analytical Shercliff and Hunt duct-flow solutions across a range of Hartmann numbers and validated against free-surface height measurements from the LMX-U experiment. To the best of our knowledge, FreeMHD2 is the first open-source, experimentally validated free-surface liquid metal solver to resolve the evolution of the induced magnetic field without invoking the inductionless approximation. By removing this approximation rather than relaxing it, the formulation provides the basis for future modeling of the finite magnetic Reynolds number conditions expected in large-scale, transient fusion events.

</details>


### [123] [Discovering a well-conditioned analytic continuation problem via dictionary learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19205)
*Thomas Chuna,Phil-Alexander Hofmann,Alexander Benedix-Robles,Tobias Dornheim*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出正则化随机优化方法(RSOM)，将解析延拓问题重新表述为字典学习问题，通过发现稀疏字典将病态逆问题映射为良态低维问题，在合成测试和真实量子蒙特卡洛数据上均取得有竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解析延拓是从虚时关联函数提取频率依赖动态量的关键但病态逆问题。现有随机方法、正则化方法和神经网络方法各有优劣但缺乏统一视角。字典学习已在多个科学领域取得惊人成果，有望为解析延拓提供新思路。

Method: 将解析延拓重新表述为字典学习问题，通过发现稀疏字典将病态逆问题映射为良态低维问题。RSOM方法融合随机方法和正则化方法的思想，学习能够稀疏表示解的字典，从而降低问题条件数。

Result: 在常见合成测试问题和有限温度电子气的真实量子蒙特卡洛数据上均取得有竞争力的结果。发现稀疏字典能够将病态逆问题映射为良态低维问题。

Conclusion: 所有随机方法和正则化方法中都存在一个字典，字典学习为未来解析延拓方法提供了新的攻击角度。该方法弥合了不同社区之间的传统分歧。

Abstract: Many fields of physics use quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) simulations to simulate quantum systems in imaginary-time $τ$ and estimate imaginary-time correlation functions (ITCF). However, extracting dynamic $ω$-dependent quantities from ITCFs is a notoriously difficult task, known as analytic continuation (AC), that amounts to solving an exponentially ill-conditioned inverse problem. Within the AC literature, there are competing stochastic and regularized approaches, as well as an emerging collection of works using parameterized models like neural networks. Here we transcend the traditional divides between the communities, introducing the regularized stochastic optimization method (RSOM). This method reformulates AC as a dictionary learning problem, discovering a sparse dictionary to represent the solution. Our approach is motivated by the astounding results dictionary learning has produced in many scientific fields. Remarkably, RSOM discovers a sparse dictionary that maps an ill-conditioned inverse problem to a low-dimensional problem that is well-conditioned. We demonstrate that the method yields competitive results for common synthetic test problems as well as for authentic QMC data from the finite temperature electron gas. This work exposes that a dictionary exists within all stochastic and regularized methods and that dictionary learning provides a new angle of attack for future AC methods.

</details>


### [124] [Acceleration of an algebraic multigrid pressure solver using graph neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19251)
*Eric Chillón,Artur K. Lidtke,Nguyen Anh Khoa Doan,Bernat Font*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图卷积同构网络（GCIN）的数据驱动代数多重网格（AMG）光滑子，通过预测最优多项式系数构建稀疏伪逆算子，在非结构化网格上加速压力-Poisson方程的求解，实现V-cycle迭代次数减少和4%-37%的加速比，且能够泛化到训练网格128倍大小的新网格及AirfRANS等工业相关问题上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化不可压缩流求解器中，压力-Poisson方程的求解是主要计算瓶颈，传统线性求解器对网格不规则性高度敏感，导致收敛缓慢。

Method: 使用改进的图卷积同构网络（GCIN）作为数据驱动AMG光滑子，图神经网络从稀疏系数矩阵中直接学习代数结构，预测最优多项式系数以构建稀疏伪逆算子，在每次V-cycle迭代中最小化残差。

Result: 在多个基准测试中减少V-cycle迭代次数，实现4%-37%的实际加速比；模型在训练网格128倍大小的网格上保持高效，并在AirfRANS数据集等未见工业问题上加速求解器收敛。

Conclusion: 所提出的数据驱动AMG光滑子能够适应非结构化网格的局部各向异性，保持求解器线性性的同时显著提升性能，并展现出良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Solving the pressure-Poisson equation remains the primary computational bottleneck in incompressible unstructured flow solvers primarily due to the inherent sensitivity of traditional linear solvers to mesh irregularities. This work introduces a data-driven algebraic multigrid (AMG) smoother that uses a modified graph convolutional isomorphism network (GCIN). The graph neural network predicts optimal polynomial coefficients to construct a sparse pseudo-inverse operator across diverse grid topologies. The coefficients are optimized to reduce the residual after each V-cycle iteration. By directly capturing the algebraic structure of the system from the sparse coefficient matrix, the proposed method maintains the solver's linearity while adapting to local anisotropies in unstructured grids. Our framework demonstrates significant performance gains by reducing the number of V-cycles required for a given tolerance and delivering wall-clock speedups from 4% to 37% across diverse benchmarks. Notably, the model exhibits robust generalization by maintaining efficiency on meshes up to 128 times larger than those seen in training, and by accelerating the solver's convergence on unseen industry-relevant problems such as the AirfRANS dataset.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [Ground state preparation of random all-to-all Hamiltonians using ADAPT-VQE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18339)
*Sabhyata Gupta,Bharath Sambasivam,Sophia E. Economou,Edwin Barnes,Alexander F. Kemper,Raghav G. Jha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了TETRIS-ADAPT-VQE算法能够为密集和稀疏SYK模型（最多N=20个马约拉纳费米子，保真度≥99.3%）以及量子SK模型（最多L=18个格点，保真度≥99.9998%）构建精确基态，但发现该算法对SK模型高效，对SYK模型（无论密集或中等稀疏）不高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机哈密顿量的基态遵循体积律纠缠，难以用张量网络建模，且是否存在能在模拟随机哈密顿量方面提供量子优势的量子算法仍是一个开放问题。

Method: 采用TETRIS-ADAPT-VQE算法来构建密集和稀疏SYK模型以及量子SK模型的基态。

Result: 对于SYK模型（N≤20），保真度≥99.3%；对于SK模型（L≤18），保真度≥99.9998%。SK模型的基态制备高效（算符池大小和电路深度方面），而SYK模型不高效。

Conclusion: TETRIS-ADAPT-VQE对SK模型有效且高效，但对SYK模型（密集和稀疏）虽能实现高保真度但效率不高，表明不同随机哈密顿量模型在量子算法上的可解性存在差异。

Abstract: The ground state of random Hamiltonians with all-to-all interactions such as the quantum Sherrington-Kirkpatrick (SK) model and the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) model follow volume-law entanglement and are expected to be hard to model using tensor networks. In recent years, some progress has been made to push the limit of classical methods using neural quantum states. However, it remains an open question whether there exist quantum algorithms that could offer a quantum advantage over the state-of-the-art classical methods in simulating random Hamiltonians. In this work, we show that one such algorithm, TETRIS-ADAPT-VQE, can construct accurate ground states for dense and sparse SYK models containing up to $N=20$ Majorana fermions achieving fidelities $\geq 99.3\%$ and for the quantum SK model with up to $L=18$ sites achieving fidelities $\geq 99.9998\%$. We find that while the preparation of ground states is efficient (in terms of operator pool size and circuit depth) for the SK model, it is not efficient for either dense or moderately sparse SYK models.

</details>


### [126] [Chaos from quantum bath fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18340)
*Ilan Baud,Tamoghna Ray,Mahaveer Prasad,Manas Kulkarni,Camille Aron*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子噪声如何在经典非混沌系统中诱导混沌。通过耗散Dicke模型（大自旋与耗散谐振模相互作用）在半经典大自旋极限下的分析，发现量子噪声能在超辐射相中产生具有分形维数和正李雅普诺夫指数的奇怪吸引子，并与剪切诱导混沌机制深度关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究环境量子涨落能否在经典非混沌系统中产生混沌，质疑传统观念中仅耗散倾向于稳定动力学，而量子涨落是否具有混沌生成能力。

Method: 以耗散Dicke模型为范式，工作在半经典大自旋极限下，通过调节经典/量子对应关系，分析量子噪声对相空间动力学的影响。

Result: 在经典规则的超辐射相中，量子噪声能产生具有分形维数和正李雅普诺夫指数的奇怪吸引子，即量子噪声诱导的混沌。

Conclusion: 揭示了量子噪声诱导混沌与数学领域发展的剪切诱导混沌机制之间的深层联系。

Abstract: The effect of a large environment on a finite-size quantum mechanical system is two-fold: It brings dissipation, but also fluctuations of thermal and quantum origin. While dissipation tends to stabilize the dynamics, we question if and how environmental quantum fluctuations can generate chaos in an otherwise classically non-chaotic system. We work out a paradigmatic model of quantum optics: the dissipative Dicke model, where a large spin interacts with a dissipative harmonic mode. We dial in the classical/quantum correspondence by working in the semiclassical regime at large but finite spin. We demonstrate that, starting from a classically regular phase space in the superradiant regime, quantum noise can generate a strange attractor with fractal dimension and a positive Lyapunov exponent. We unveil the deep connection with shear-induced chaos that was recently developed in the mathematical community.

</details>


### [127] [Steady-state spectral kissing and dissipative phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18348)
*Devesh Karthik,Jorge Chávez-Carlos,Edson M. Signor,Victor S. Batista,Francisco Pérez-Bernal,Lea F. Santos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现Kerr参量振荡器（KPO）中的“光谱亲吻”（能级对合并）现象在耗散稳态密度矩阵谱中存在对应物，并在弱耗散条件下推导了稳态光谱亲吻的出现与消失的临界线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索闭系统激发态量子相变（ESQPT）的光谱亲吻现象在开放耗散系统中的对应表现，并揭示其随耗散强度变化的演化规律。

Method: 以耗散KPO为模型，分析弱耗散条件下稳态密度矩阵本征值的准简并对结构；通过经典极限分析推导光谱亲吻出现和消失的临界线解析表达式。

Result: 弱耗散时稳态密度矩阵本征值呈现与闭系统光谱亲吻镜像对应的准简并对；随耗散增强该配对逐渐消失；给出了控制稳态光谱亲吻出现与耗散相变消失的临界线解析式。

Conclusion: 光谱亲吻现象在耗散系统中存在稳态对应，该对应受耗散强度调控，并在经典极限下可解析描述其临界行为。

Abstract: Spectral kissing, recently realized in a Kerr parametric oscillator (KPO), refers to the merging of pairs of energy levels and arises as a manifestation of an excited-state quantum phase transition (ESQPT). Here, we show that this phenomenon has a dissipative counterpart encoded in the spectrum of the steady-state density matrix. Using a dissipative KPO as a representative example, we demonstrate that, in the weak-dissipation regime, the eigenvalues of the steady-state density matrix organize into quasi-degenerate pairs that mirror the spectral kissing of the corresponding closed system. As the dissipation strength increases, this pairing gradually disappears. By analyzing the classical limit of the system, we derive analytical expressions for the critical lines governing both the onset of steady-state spectral kissing and its disappearance at a dissipative phase transition.

</details>


### [128] [All Reflective Field-widened Unbalanced Interferometer for Quantum Sensing and Communication Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18358)
*Ramy Tannous,Dogan Sinar,Tabitha D. Arulpragasam,Thomas Jennewein*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅由反射面实现、无需波前校正的宽视场干涉仪，可在空间多模光束中实现高可见度（>0.97）的时域编码信号干涉，适用于卫星通信等湍流光信道中的量子系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时域编码信号在自由空间光信道中因大气湍流导致波前畸变，需要自适应光学等严格的模式滤波。为降低系统复杂性，探索无需波前校正的无源多模接收方案。

Method: 设计了一种由球面凹面镜和平面镜构成的腔型成像系统，实现宽视场干涉仪，仅使用反射表面，具有小体积、消色差特性，并基于标准球面镜。

Result: 实验实现了对空间多模光束的高可见度干涉（大于0.97）。

Conclusion: 该干涉仪适用于空间多模及湍流光信道，如卫星通信，并适用于采用时域编码量子比特的量子系统。

Abstract: Interference of time-bin encoded signals over free-space optical channels typically requires stringent mode filtering on receivers due to wavefront distortions from atmospheric turbulence, conventionally addressed with adaptive optics. Passive multimode receivers based on field-widened interferometers present a compelling alternative, enabling direct interference without the overhead of wavefront correction. We demonstrate a field-widened interferometer design that is implemented solely with reflective surfaces and achieves a high interference visibility (greater than 0.97) for spatially multimode beams. The interference of the multimode beams is enabled by two imaging systems that consist of a cavity configuration between a spherical concave mirror and a flat mirror. The configuration enables small form-factors, is inherently achromatic, and is based on standard spherical mirrors which reduces the complexity of the system. The interferometer is applicable for spatially multimode and turbulent optical channels, such as satellite communication, and is designed for quantum systems that use time-bin encoded qubits.

</details>


### [129] [Equilibration of generalized subsystems: a quantum-channel approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18360)
*Pedro S. Correia,Adalberto D. Varizi,Gabriel Dias Carvalho*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于广义子系统的统一态级框架，将量子系统中的环境子系统和受限测量两种平衡化情景纳入同一描述，证明了当广义子系统的维度远小于被丢弃的微观信息有效维度时，系统会达到平衡，且该平衡对初始微观细节不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量子平衡化结果通常分别处理两种情形：系统-环境结构（复合系统幺正演化而目标子系统平衡）和受限测量（如粗粒化POVM和可观测量导致测量统计量平衡）。作者希望将这些描述统一到一个共同的态级框架中。

Method: 引入广义子系统的概念，将可访问的有效态视为量子信道作用于微观态的输出。推导了广义子系统平衡的界，并分析了典型初始态在大子空间中的行为。

Result: 证明了当广义子系统的维度远小于被丢弃的微观信息有效维度时，系统会平衡；该条件对于大子空间中的典型初始态成立；平衡描述对微观初始细节基本不敏感；该框架能够恢复普通子系统和有限族POVM的平衡界。通过有限分辨率能量通道的例子，展示了残留有效相干的显式表达及谱多重性对相干的约束作用。

Conclusion: 该工作为一般形式的有限可访问信息下的量子平衡化提供了一个统一的态级表述。

Abstract: Quantum systems governed by unitary and reversible microscopic dynamics may nevertheless exhibit equilibration, in the sense that some effective description becomes time-independent. Standard equilibration results usually consider two separate situations: system-environment structures, in which the composite system evolves unitarily while the system of interest equilibrates, and restricted measurements, such as coarse-grained POVMs and observables, in which the measurement statistics equilibrate. Here, we bring these descriptions into a common state-level framework using the concept of generalized subsystems, where the accessible effective state appears as the output of a quantum channel acting on the microscopic state. We derive bounds showing that generalized subsystems equilibrate when their dimension is small compared with the effective dimension of the discarded microscopic information. We further show that this condition is met for typical initial states in large subspaces and that the resulting equilibrium description is largely insensitive to microscopic initial details. The framework recovers the usual equilibration bounds for ordinary subsystems and finite families of POVMs. As an example, we also introduce a finite-resolution energy channel that maps unresolved microscopic energy levels into effective energy levels, thereby making residual effective coherences explicit and showing how spectral multiplicities constrain those coherences while strengthening equilibration. Our results provide a unified state-level formulation of quantum equilibration under general forms of limited accessible information.

</details>


### [130] [Universal entanglement probes of topological order and locally-achiral manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18361)
*Yarden Sheffer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出通过基态波函数的体纠缠提取拓扑序信息的方法，证明了在满足“局部手性缺失”条件的流形上可以提取拓扑配分函数，并展示了该方法能探测超越S和T矩阵的2+1d拓扑相普适性质，以及4维中超越上同调的T-SPT序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前人工作表明可以从多熵度量中提取部分普适信息，但尚不清楚这类纠缠度量能在多大程度上从基态提取任意普适信息。本文旨在回答并拓展这一问题的边界。

Method: 定义“局部手性缺失”流形的拓扑条件，证明在该条件下可从多副本纠缠度量中提取拓扑配分函数Z(M)；通过Pontryagin数归零性质关联4维T-SPT序，并构造相应的纠缠度量。

Result: 证明了局部手性缺失流形可提取超越S和T矩阵的2+1d拓扑相信息；4维中此类流形Pontryagin数为零；给出了探测4维超越上同调T-SPT序的纠缠度量。

Conclusion: 体纠缠度量可用于提取更丰富的拓扑序普适信息，为分类局部手性缺失流形和探测新型拓扑序提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We consider the problem of identifying a topological order based on bulk entanglement of the ground-state wavefunction. Previous work showed that some universal information can be extracted from multi-entropy measures, a class of multipartite entanglement measures obtained by applying permutation operators exchanging the degrees of freedom between different replicas of the wavefunction. It remains an open question to what extent such entanglement measures can be used to extract any universal information from the ground state. Here we show that the topological partition function $Z(M)$ of a manifold $M$ can be extracted provided that $M$ satisfies a topological condition which we term ``local achirality". We show that locally-achiral manifolds can be used to extract universal properties of 2+1d topological phases that go beyond the $S$ and $T$ matrices. As a first step towards classifying locally-achiral manifolds, we show that, in four dimensions, such manifolds have vanishing Pontryagin number. We relate this property to the existence of beyond-cohomology time-reversal symmetry protected topological order (T-SPT) in four dimensions. Finally, we present an entanglement measure that detects this nontrivial T-SPT.

</details>


### [131] [Characterization of nested Walsh parity-check filters in a single-photon eight-mode register on a cloud photonic processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18408)
*Emma Tully,Jonathan Washburn,Megan Simons*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Quandela的Belenos云端光量子处理器上，实现了两种嵌套的Walsh奇偶校验滤波器，基于单光子八模空间寄存器（由立方体Q₃顶点索引），实验验证了泄漏抑制和综合征路由，而非纠错或抗光子损失。在超过34万次探测中，中性输入的直流端口泄漏为0.02%-1.1%（均值0.6%），相对于理想基线抑制约21倍，相对于非中性对照抑制31.6倍。三个面奇偶综合征通道的选择性达94%-99%。保持扇区的幺正核心在一至三次应用中使泄漏远低于非中性对照，差异主要由校准和编译系统误差主导。报告了Hong-Ou-Mandel退化事件，其中抑制消失并在重新校准后恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在光量子计算中，路径/模式编码是实现量子信息处理的重要方式，但编码空间中的泄漏（leakage）和综合征路由（syndrome routing）的精确验证尚不充分。本文旨在通过实验表征Walsh奇偶校验滤波器在光量子处理器上的性能，为后续基于第一量子化编码的量子计算提供基准和系统误差分析。

Method: 实验使用Quandela的Belenos云端光量子处理器，基于单光子八模空间寄存器（模式由立方体Q₃顶点索引），实现了两种嵌套滤波器：经典[8,7,2]单奇偶校验码的零和子空间𝒩，以及[8,4,4]扩展汉明码的奇偶校验子空间𝒮⊂𝒩，包含一个DC通道和三个面奇偶综合征通道。通过后选择单光子探测，测量泄漏抑制、综合征路由选择性、保持扇区的幺正核心性能，并量化校准偏移、编译散布和Hong-Ou-Mandel退化等系统效应。

Result: 超过34万次探测显示：中性输入残差DC端口泄漏0.02%-1.1%（均值0.6%），相对于理想0.125 DC捕获基线抑制约21倍，相对于非中性对照抑制31.6倍。注入DC污染产生单调软错误信号。三个面奇偶综合征通道以94%-99%的选择性路由到预测端口。保持扇区的幺正核心在一至三次应用中使泄漏远低于非中性对照，差异主要由校准和编译系统误差主导，而非门周期物理。量化了固定模式分离器偏差、±0.02校准偏移和近10⁻³泄漏水平的编译散布。报告了一次Hong-Ou-Mandel退化事件，抑制消失并在重新校准后恢复。

Conclusion: 本文成功在光量子处理器上实验验证了基于经典纠错码的Walsh奇偶校验滤波器的泄漏抑制和综合征路由功能，展示了路径/模式编码在光量子信息处理中的可行性。系统误差分析揭示了当前校准和编译精度的主要限制，为未来实现真正的量子纠错和抗光子损失编码提供了重要实验基准。

Abstract: We characterize two nested Walsh parity-check filters implemented on Quandela's Belenos cloud photonic processor in a single-photon eight-mode spatial register. The modes are indexed by the vertices of the cube $Q_3$. The filters realize the classical $[8,7,2]$ single-parity-check code, the zero-sum neutral subspace $\mathcal{N}$ and the $[8,4,4]$ extended Hamming code, the parity-checked subspace $\mathcal{S}\subset\mathcal{N}$ with one DC and three face-parity syndrome channels. These are first-quantized path/mode encodings of classical codes: the experiment verifies leakage suppression and syndrome routing, not error correction or protection against photon loss, and all probabilities are conditional on postselected single-photon detections. Across more than 340,000 detections, neutral inputs show residual DC-port leakage of $0.02\%$-$1.1\%$ (mean $0.6\%$), corresponding to $\approx21\times$ suppression relative to the ideal $0.125$ DC-capture baseline and $31.6\times$ relative to the measured non-neutral control. Injected DC contamination gives a monotonic soft error signal, and the three face-parity syndrome channels route to their predicted ports with $94$-$99\%$ selectivity. A sector-preserving unitary core keeps leakage far below non-neutral controls over one to three applications, with differences dominated by calibration and compilation systematics rather than gate-cycle physics. We quantify these limits, including fixed-pattern separator bias, $\pm 0.02$ calibration offsets, and compilation scatter near the $10^{-3}$ leakage level, and report a Hong-Ou-Mandel degradation episode in which suppression vanished and recovered after recalibration.

</details>


### [132] [Entanglement response to Temperature in Interacting Two-Qubit Thermal States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18415)
*Zain H. Saleem,Iram Saleem*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了两量子比特热态中纠缠对温度变化的响应，推导了热 concurrence、其导数及热量子Fisher信息的精确表达式，证明了热纠缠变化率受热量子Fisher信息约束，并建立了纠缠曲率与热量子Fisher信息之间的界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究相互作用的两量子比特热态中纠缠对温度变化的响应机制，以及热量子Fisher信息如何约束纠缠的温度敏感性。

Method: 针对一般两量子比特相互作用哈密顿量，推导了热 concurrence、其对逆温度的一阶和二阶导数、以及热量子Fisher信息的精确解析表达式。

Result: 热纠缠的变化率受热量子Fisher信息约束；纠缠曲率与热量子Fisher信息之间存在界限关系；温度不确定性导致的纠缠损失受同一热量子Fisher信息约束。

Conclusion: 热量子Fisher信息是约束两量子比特热态中纠缠响应与鲁棒性的基本量，它同时决定热计量灵敏度与纠缠的温度稳定性。

Abstract: We investigate the response of entanglement to temperature variations in interacting two-qubit thermal states. For a general two-qubit interaction Hamiltonian, we derive exact expressions for the thermal concurrence, its first and second derivatives with respect to inverse temperature, and the thermal quantum Fisher information. We show that the rate of change of thermal entanglement is bounded by the thermal quantum Fisher information. We further derive a bound relating entanglement curvature and thermal quantum Fisher information, and show that temperature uncertainty induces a loss of entanglement bounded by the same quantity that determines thermometric sensitivity. These results establish thermal quantum Fisher information as a fundamental constraint on the response and robustness of entanglement in interacting two-qubit thermal states.

</details>


### [133] [Gatekeepers and Hallucinations: A Layered Evaluation Framework for LLM-Driven Quantum Circuit Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18422)
*Christopher Coleman,Sharon Marfatia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一个针对量子模拟工作流中大语言模型的层次化评估框架，包含门控筛选、电路保真度分析和设计熵三个层面，揭示了五种LLM故障模式，并发现部分模型失败实际源于评估平台本身的静默回退模板替换问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM嵌入量子模拟工作流（IDE协作者、笔记本助手、智能体管道），评估必须超越功能性正确性，在结构化错误通过昂贵管道传播之前提前发现和捕获它们。

Method: 提出了三层评估框架：(i) 基于七项物理和框架标准的门控筛选词表；(ii) 电路保真度分析，比较模型输出与H2/STO-3G/Jordan-Wigner/UCCSD的分析解和参考实现值，包含ansatz分类和门组合分解；(iii) 设计熵，一种运行间行为一致性指标。此外还对评估平台自身源码进行了取证审计。

Result: 归纳了五种不同的LLM故障模式（几何幻觉、不存在的API使用、运行时集成失败、约束违反、看似合理但不可验证的输出），发现两个明显的模型失败实际源于测试平台的静默回退模板替换，表明评估基础设施与它测试的模型应处于同一信任边界内。

Conclusion: 在多个基础模型上的Materials Project集成管道应用表明，门控式验证对于可靠部署是必要的，而非可选的。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) become embedded in quantum simulation workflows (IDE copilots, notebook assistants, agentic pipelines), evaluation must move beyond functional correctness to anticipate and catch structured failures before they propagate through expensive pipelines. We present a layered evaluation framework for materials-informed Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) circuit generation: (i) a gatekeeper screening rubric across seven physical and framework criteria; (ii) a circuit fidelity analysis comparing model outputs against analytical and reference-implementation values for H2/STO-3G/Jordan-Wigner/UCCSD, with ansatz classification and gate-composition breakdown; and (iii) design entropy, a run-to-run behavioral consistency metric. We surface a taxonomy of five distinct LLM failure modes (geometry hallucination, nonexistent API usage, runtime integration failures, constraint violations, and plausible-but-unverifiable output), each with distinct detectability profiles and structural to the task rather than to any one model. A forensic audit of the evaluation platform's own source code further establishes that two apparent model failures originated in the harness through silent fallback-template substitution, demonstrating that evaluation infrastructure belongs inside the same trust boundary as the models it tests. Applied across multiple foundation models on a Materials Project integrated pipeline, the framework shows that gatekeeper-style validation is necessary, not optional, for reliable deployment.

</details>


### [134] [Quantum algorithm for Valiant-Vazirani reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18428)
*Patrick Kelly,Victoria S. Ordonez,Michael R. Geller*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究如何利用非线性量子计算（扭转模型）解决NP问题。通过Valiant-Vazirani定理的滤波预言机，将SAT问题随机多项式归约为UNIQUE SAT，再使用扭转非线性在多项式时间内求解UNIQUE SAT，从而实现NP问题的多项式时间求解（但不包括#P问题）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Abrams和Lloyd曾指出非线性薛定谔方程可用于在多项式时间内求解NP和#P问题，但实际实现中仅能获得模拟或涌现非线性（如扭转模型）。然而扭转模型在解决SAT问题上存在不足，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构造Valiant-Vazirani定理的滤波预言机，实现从SAT到UNIQUE SAT的随机多项式时间归约；然后利用扭转非线性在无噪声极限下多项式时间内求解UNIQUE SAT。

Result: 在无噪声理想条件下，通过量子Valiant-Vazirani归约结合扭转非线性，可实现NP问题的多项式时间求解；但该量子归约并不比经典版本更快，且无法解决#P问题。

Conclusion: 非线性量子计算（扭转模型）结合容错实现，可多项式时间内求解NP问题，但对#P问题无能为力。量子Valiant-Vazirani归约在速度上不优于经典版本。

Abstract: There is growing interest in extensions of the standard model of gate-based quantum computation to include auxiliary degrees of freedom evolving according to a nonlinear Schrödinger equation. By reducing the Boolean satisfiability problem SAT to quantum state discrimination, Abrams and Lloyd argued that the right type of nonlinearity can be used to solve {\sf NP} and \#{\sf P} problems in polynomial time, at least in an idealized noise-free limit. For practical implementation, however, we are restricted to simulated and emergent nonlinearities, such as that appearing in mean field models for ultracold atoms and similar ensembles. A prominent example is the torsion model, which arises in two-component Bose-Einstein condensates and spin models with all-to-all Ising interaction. But torsion-based state discrimination appears to fall short of solving SAT. Here we close this gap by constructing the filtered oracle of the Valiant-Vazirani theorem, providing a randomized polynomial-time reduction from SAT to UNIQUE SAT, a promise problem where there is at most 1 satisfying assignment. In the noise free limit, the UNIQUE SAT problem can be olved in polynomial time using torsion nonlinearity. Quantum Valiant-Vazirani reduction is no faster than the efficient classical version, but a fault-tolerant implementation coupled to a nonlinear quantum coprocessor simulating torsion would enable polynomial time solution to {\sf NP} (but not \#{\sf P}) problems.

</details>


### [135] [Noncyclic geometric phase in three-level Ramsey interferometry for enhanced metrology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18443)
*Zhifan Zhou,Yaxin Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出三能级拉姆齐干涉测量方法，利用非循环几何相位响应增强相位传感，通过近测地线闭合跃迁实现小累积相位向大读出相位转换，在技术噪声限制下提供信噪比增益，并通过调节初始相位偏移实现投影稳定性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统两能级拉姆齐干涉仪中，测量相位随询问时间线性累积，灵敏度受限。本文旨在利用三能级系统的非循环几何相位响应来突破这一限制，实现相位传感的增强。

Method: 引入三能级拉姆齐干涉测量法，利用非循环几何相位响应；通过投影内路径干涉重塑从累积信号相位到读出相位的映射；在近测地线闭合跃迁附近实现小相位信号到大幅读出相位偏移的放大。

Result: 量化了增益-可见度权衡，识别出在技术噪声限制条件下能提供净信噪比增益的有限工作窗口；通过调节初始拉姆齐相位偏移，可将高斜率窗口定位到期望工作点并短周期重复采样，实现投影稳定性的提升。

Conclusion: 建立了多能级拉姆齐路线以增强量子平台中的相位灵敏度，其中两个信号收集内路径干涉产生非循环几何响应，为改进相位测量提供几何捷径。

Abstract: In a standard two-level Ramsey interferometer, the measured phase accumulates linearly during the interrogation time. Here, we introduce three-level Ramsey interferometry that employs a noncyclic geometric phase response to enhance phase sensing, with projected internal-path interference reshaping the mapping from accumulated signal phase to readout phase. Near a geodesic-closure transition, a small accumulated signal phase produces a sharply amplified readout-phase shift. We quantify the accompanying gain--visibility tradeoff and identify a finite operating window in which the amplified response yields a net signal-to-noise-ratio gain under technical-noise-limited conditions. By tuning an initial Ramsey phase offset, this high-slope window can be positioned at a desired operating point and sampled repeatedly with shorter cycles, providing a geometric shortcut to improved projected stability. More broadly, these results establish a multilevel Ramsey route to enhanced phase sensitivity in quantum platforms, where two signal-collecting internal paths interfere to produce a noncyclic geometric response.

</details>


### [136] [Exact propagating Dirac wave packets in an attractive Coulomb-like potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18470)
*Siddhant Das*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在轴对称势 $V=-v_0/\rho$ 中构造了狄拉克方程的首个精确、正能量、可归一化的波包解，发现其概率密度与自旋方向完全解耦，且在临界耦合 $v_0 \to \hbar c/2$ 处时间演化完全冻结。此外，还给出了从2D亥姆霍兹方程映射到狄拉克波包的简单方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 狄拉克方程在外势场中精确的、正能量的可归一化波包解此前尚未被构造出来，作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在轴对称势 $V=-v_0/\rho$ 中求解狄拉克方程，构造波包解；并利用2D亥姆霍兹方程的解映射生成更多精确狄拉克波包。

Result: 得到了首个该类解，其中一族仅由初等函数构成，纵剖面在非相对论极限下再现自由薛定谔Hermite-Gauss波包；所有波包的概率密度与自旋方向点状解耦；在临界耦合 $v_0\to\hbar c/2$ 处时间演化完全冻结。

Conclusion: 成功构造了狄拉克方程在特定外势下的精确波包解，揭示了自旋-轨道耦合下概率密度与自旋解耦的反直觉现象，以及临界耦合下的动力学冻结效应。

Abstract: We construct exact, positive-energy, normalizable wave-packet solutions of the Dirac equation in the axisymmetric potential $V=-\,v_0/ρ$ -- to our knowledge, the first such solutions in any external potential. Remarkably, one family comprises only elementary functions whose longitudinal profiles reproduce the free-Schrödinger Hermite--Gauss wave packets in the nonrelativistic limit. All packets share two striking features: (i) a probability density that is pointwise decoupled from spin orientation -- despite the inherent spin-orbit coupling of the Dirac equation -- and (ii) a complete freezing of their time evolution at the critical coupling $v_0\to\hbar c/2$. We also present a simple scheme that maps solutions of the 2D Helmholtz equation to further exact Dirac wave packets.

</details>


### [137] [Towards an Optimally Distributed Quantum Fourier Transform Circuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18494)
*Zachary Vernec,Michael Silver,Hans-Arno Jacobsen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于最优门打包的量子傅里叶变换（QFT）电路分区方案，用于分布式量子计算中最小化e-bit数量，并与先前的解析方案及通用电路分区算法进行了比较，最后在量子硬件上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展量子计算的一个有前景的方向是通过生成纠缠来连接量子处理单元（QPU），这需要电路分区——即利用量子隐形传态协议将量子电路部分重写以在分布式量子系统上运行，同时保持电路实现的酉操作。分区时需最小化的关键指标是e-bit数量（QPU之间必须生成的最大纠缠量子对数量）。

Method: 专注于对量子傅里叶变换（QFT）电路进行分区，提出了一种基于最优门打包的分区方案，并与先前的解析分区方案以及通用电路分区算法进行对比，最后在量子硬件上实现分区电路进行验证。

Result: 基于最优门打包的分区方案在e-bit计数上优于先前的解析分区方案和通用电路分区算法，并在量子硬件上得到了实验验证。

Conclusion: 该研究为分布式量子计算中的QFT电路分区提供了更优的方案，有助于扩展量子计算系统。

Abstract: A promising avenue for scaling quantum computing is to connect quantum processing units (QPUs) by generating entanglement between them. This requires circuit partitioning: partially rewriting quantum circuits to run on a distributed quantum system using quantum teleportation protocols, while preserving the unitary operation implemented by the circuit. The key metric to minimize when partitioning is the e-bit count, defined as the number of maximally entangled qubit pairs that must be generated between QPUs. We focus on partitioning the quantum Fourier transform (QFT) circuit, which is widely used as a subroutine in quantum algorithms such as quantum phase estimation and arithmetic circuits. Specifically, we present a partitioning scheme based on optimal gate-packing, compare it against prior analytical partitioning schemes for the QFT, and evaluate it against partitions produced by general-purpose circuit partitioning algorithms. We further validate our approach by implementing the partitioned circuit on quantum hardware.

</details>


### [138] [Exponentially many initializations to avoid barren plateaus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18515)
*Ankit Kulshrestha,Ricard Puig,Diego García-Martín,Lukasz Cincio,Ilya Safro,Zoë Holmes,M. Cerezo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现避开贫瘠高原的初始化策略并非唯一，存在指数级多的不等价初始化族，且不同策略会导致不同的优化结果，将指数浓度问题转化为选择可训练口袋的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有观点认为贫瘠高原是平均情况现象，可通过更仔细的参数初始化来避免。但本文认为情况更为微妙，需要系统分析初始化策略是否真的能摆脱完全集中的贫瘠高原不动点。

Method: 提出一阶矩框架（first-moment framework），提供算子级别的诊断工具来判断初始化策略能否避免贫瘠高原。通过分析不同初始化策略（如恒等初始化、高斯初始化、偏移、偏置和非对称分布）的一阶矩性质，找出其行为差异。

Result: 恢复了已有的初始化方案，但发现避免贫瘠高原高度非唯一，存在指数级多的不等价初始化族。不同一阶矩不同的初始化策略会导致不同的最终收敛最小值。

Conclusion: 通过智能初始化避免贫瘠高原，实际上是将指数浓度问题转化为选择正确可训练口袋（trainable pocket）的挑战，而非一劳永逸的解决方案。

Abstract: Barren plateaus are stated as an average-case phenomenon: pick an ansatz, initialize it naively, and concentration follows. This has led to the common view that a potential cure for barren plateaus is simply to initialize the parameters more carefully. Here we show that the situation is subtler. We introduce a first-moment framework that gives a simple operator-level diagnostic for when an initialization may escape the fully concentrated barren-plateau fixed point, and for comparing the biases induced by different initialization strategies. Our framework recovers several known initialization schemes such as identity and Gaussian initialization, but also shows that barren-plateau avoidance is highly non-unique. Indeed, many shifted, biased, and non-symmetric parameter distributions can avoid concentration, and these choices need not be equivalent. In fact, our results show that one can generate exponentially many families of inequivalent initialization strategies. Then, our numerics indicate that different first-moment-distinct initializations can lead to different attained minima, suggesting that avoiding barren plateaus via smart initializations can trade the exponential concentration problem for the challenge of selecting the right trainable pocket amongst many options.

</details>


### [139] [Ground- and excited-state energies extraction via Trotterization on IBM quantum computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18534)
*Fernando Espinoza-Ortiz,Chungwei Lin,Chih-Chun Chien*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在IBM量子计算机上通过Trotterized时间演化算符实现Hadamard测试，同时提取横场伊辛模型(TFIM)和横纵场伊辛模型(TLFIM)的基态和激发态能量。对于TFIM，Trotter化电路允许任意时间的恒定深度电路(CDC)，可定位多达六自旋的大量本征能量。通过电路综合，发现三自旋TLFIM具有恒定深度结构，尽管不满足已知CDC准则。CDC通过动力学提取三站点TLFIM的基态和第一激发态能量。还处理了噪声背景和离散傅里叶变换带来的复杂性，并比较了不同代IBM硬件的结果以突出改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用量子计算机通过Hadamard测试同时提取量子多体系统的基态和激发态能量，解决传统方法在噪声环境下提取能谱的可靠性问题。

Method: 在IBM量子计算机上实现Hadamard测试结合Trotterized时间演化算符；针对TFIM和TLFIM构建恒定深度电路(CDC)；采用电路综合方法；结合离散傅里叶变换和噪声背景处理技术增强提取可靠性。

Result: TFIM的Trotterization电路支持任意时间的CDC，可提取多达六自旋的大量本征能量；三自旋TLFIM虽不满足已知CDC准则但具有恒定深度结构；成功提取了三站点TLFIM的基态和第一激发态能量；不同代IBM硬件的结果显示了性能改进。

Conclusion: CDC方法可有效从量子动力学中提取多体系统的能谱，即使对于不满足传统CDC准则的模型也可通过电路综合实现恒定深度，硬件代际升级显著提升了提取可靠性。

Abstract: We implement the Hadamard test with Trotterized time-evolution operators on IBM quantum computers to simultaneously extract ground- and excited-state energies of the transverse field Ising model (TFIM) and transverse longitudinal field Ising model (TLFIM). The Trotterization circuits for the TFIM admit constant-depth circuits (CDCs) for arbitrary time, allowing us to locate a large number of eigen-energies above the background noise for up to six spins. Via circuit synthesis we show that the three-spin TLFIM has constant-depth structure although it does not meet the known CDC criteria. The CDCs enable the extraction of the ground and first-excited state energies of the three-site TLFIM via its dynamics. We also address complications from the noisy background and discrete Fourier transform to enhance the reliability of the extraction process and compare the results from different generations of IBM hardware to highlight the improvement.

</details>


### [140] [Towards Entanglement-Enhanced Atom Interferometry Using Bow-Tie Cavities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18552)
*Christian Mancini,Marco Malitesta,Tommaso Mariani,Annalisa Pappalardo,Giuseppe Vinelli,Paolo Vezio,Gabriele Rosi,Enrico Meli,Leonardo Salvi,Guglielmo Maria Tino*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种用于锶原子干涉仪的 monolithic bow-tie 光学腔，该腔具有高机械稳定性、高精细度（F=5.7×10^4）以及均匀的原子-光耦合特性，预计可通过腔反馈压缩和量子非破坏测量分别实现24 dB和28 dB的自旋压缩，为下一代量子增强原子干涉仪提供了平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子干涉仪的性能受限于量子投影噪声，而通过腔辅助产生纠缠态是实现超越标准量子极限的量子增强干涉测量的有前景途径。现有驻波法布里-珀罗腔存在耦合不均匀的问题，亟需一种适用于自由下落原子、具有均匀耦合的腔体结构。

Method: 研制了一种 monolithic bow-tie 腔，采用行波几何结构而非传统驻波结构，实现原子-光耦合在整个原子系综上的均匀性；腔体工作于锶原子689 nm跃迁线，精细度达5.7×10^4，支持两个焦斑（腰斑分别为164 μm和31 μm），可实现不同耦合 regime。

Result: 腔的精细度F=5.7×10^4，单镜透射率足够高以实现高效原子信息提取；在包含10^5个原子的系综中，预计通过腔反馈压缩可实现24 dB自旋压缩，通过量子非破坏测量可实现28 dB自旋压缩。

Conclusion: 该 bow-tie 腔具有高机械稳定性、抗失调鲁棒性、良好的光学与原子接入性，并支持多种压缩态生成策略，是下一代量子增强原子干涉仪的理想平台。

Abstract: Atom interferometers are among the most sensitive instruments for precision measurements and tests of fundamental physics. Their performance, however, is ultimately limited by quantum projection noise when uncorrelated atomic ensembles are employed. Cavity-assisted generation of entangled states has proven to be a promising route toward quantum-enhanced interferometry beyond the standard quantum limit. In this work, we present the realization and characterization of a monolithic bow-tie cavity developed to achieve a strong collective atom-light coupling with strontium atoms. Unlike conventional standing-wave Fabry-Pérot resonators, the traveling-wave geometry of the bow-tie cavity provides homogeneous atom-light coupling over the entire atomic ensemble, making it particularly suitable for entanglement-enhanced atom interferometry with freely falling atoms. The monolithic cavity architecture presents several scientifically relevant features such as high mechanical stability, high finesse, robustness against mirror misalignment, optical and atomic access and the option of generating squeezed states through different strategies. The cavity was realized for operation on the strontium $(5s^2) ^1S_0-(5s5p) ^3P_1$ transition at 689 nm and achieves a finesse of $\mathcal{F}=5.7\times 10^4$ while keeping the transmission of a single mirror sufficiently large to allow for efficient atomic information extraction. In this geometry, the cavity supports two foci with waists of 164 $μ$m and 31 $μ$m which gives access to different regimes of atom-cavity coupling. For ensembles containing up to $10^5$ atoms, the cavity is expected to enable metrological gains approaching 24 dB of spin squeezing through cavity-feedback squeezing, and 28 dB through quantum non-demolition measurements, demonstrating its potential as a platform for next-generation quantum-enhanced atom interferometers.

</details>


### [141] [Separation of Statistical Complexity and Trainability in Variational Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18580)
*Suman Mandal,Maximillian Daughtry,Eduardo R. Mucciolo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究表明，变分量子算法中常见的统计复杂性诊断指标（如Porter-Thomas统计、纠缠谱能级统计、逆参与比）在浅层结构化电路中会呈现类似随机态或随机矩阵的行为，但这并不意味着梯度消失（贫瘠高原）。电路深度增加时这些指标趋近随机行为，但变分优化仍有效，无指数级梯度抑制。这揭示了有限深度变分电路中不同复杂性方面（谱统计与全局态随机化）的分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战“电路表达性增加必然导致可训练性丧失（贫瘠高原）”这一广泛接受的直觉。现有理论表明足够随机的电路会导致贫瘠高原，但作者想探究随机性统计特征是否必须在贫瘠高原出现之后才会显现，还是可以在可训练性保持的情况下提前出现。

Method: 以一维cluster-Ising模型和广义toric code哈密顿量为例，使用结构化变分电路。通过分析Porter-Thomas统计、纠缠谱能级统计和逆参与比来刻画态复杂性，并监测变分优化过程中的梯度行为。

Result: 在两种模型中，增加电路深度使统计诊断趋近随机态/随机矩阵行为，但变分优化仍然有效，未发现指数级梯度抑制。谱相关性在较浅深度即可通过局部生成结构发展，而全局态随机化及相关的测度集中效应尚未实现。

Conclusion: 常用的统计复杂性诊断本身不能决定可训练性；在有限深度变分电路中，不同方面的复杂性（如谱统计与全局随机化）存在分离。这暗示需重新审视评估VQA可训练性的诊断指标，并强调了局域性在理解这一分离中的关键作用。

Abstract: Variational quantum algorithms (VQAs) are among the leading approaches for near-term quantum computing, yet their performance can degrade in barren plateau regimes characterized by vanishing gradients. A widely held intuition is that increasing circuit expressivity, often associated with random-state behavior, leads to a loss of trainability. Existing results show that sufficiently random circuits can lead to barren plateaus. Here we show that standard statistical signatures of randomness can emerge well before this regime, without degrading trainability. We demonstrate this behavior in structured variational circuits applied to the one-dimensional cluster-Ising model and a generalized toric code Hamiltonian. To characterize state complexity, we analyze Porter-Thomas statistics, entanglement-spectrum level statistics, and inverse participation ratios. Across both models, increasing circuit depth drives these diagnostics toward random-state-like or random-matrix-like behavior, while variational optimization remains effective, with no evidence of exponential gradient suppression in the regime studied. We interpret this behavior in terms of locality. Spectral correlations develop at relatively shallow depth through locally generated structure, while global state randomization and the associated concentration-of-measure effects are not yet realized. These results show that commonly used statistical diagnostics of complexity do not by themselves determine trainability. Instead, they point to a separation between different aspects of complexity in finite-depth variational circuits.

</details>


### [142] [The quantum-advantage resource in multimode OPA light: Identification, optimization, extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18605)
*Vitaly Kocharovsky,Kunwar Kalra*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并揭示了混合多模高斯态中量子复杂度资源的性质，基于凸优化、多模光子数统计、Hafnian主定理和#P-hard复杂度，为量子优势提供了普适量化表征。通过脉冲OPA在非绝热条件下生成数千个多体纠缠压缩模式，并优化提取，展示了比Bloch-Messiah超模更现实的度量，开辟了量子信息科学新应用，如3D簇态生成和量子优势验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子复杂度资源度量缺乏对混合多模高斯态的普适量化表征，需要一种更现实的度量方法来指导多模OPA的设计，以推动量子信息科学中的实际应用。

Method: 基于凸优化、多模光子数统计、Hafnian主定理和#P-hard复杂度理论，建立量子复杂度资源的量化框架；通过脉冲OPA在非线性、时空非绝热条件下生成并优化提取数千个多体纠缠压缩模式。

Result: 提出的量子复杂度资源度量比传统Bloch-Messiah超模方法更现实，能够在脉冲OPA中生成数千个多体纠缠压缩模式，可用于生成3D簇态和展示量子优势。

Conclusion: 该量子复杂度资源度量能够有效指导多模OPA的设计和优化，为量子信息科学中的重要应用（如单向量子计算的3D簇态生成和量子优势验证）提供了新路径。

Abstract: We introduce the notion and reveal remarkable properties of quantum complexity resource contained in a mixed multimode Gaussian state and providing universal quantitative characterization of its quantum advantage. The notion is based on convex optimization, multimode photon number statistics, Hafnian Master Theorem, and #P-hard complexity. We consider pulsed OPAs targeting maximal quantum complexity resource and thousands of multipartite-entangled squeezed modes of output light via nonlinear, spatio-temporally nonadiabatic generation inside OPA and optimized extraction out of OPA. We show that such figure of merit is more realistic than Bloch--Messiah supermodes and guides to multimode OPAs opening new paths to important applications in quantum information science such as generation of 3D cluster states for one-way photonic quantum computing and demonstration of quantum advantage.

</details>


### [143] [Characterization of three-qubit controlled unitary gates of Schmidt rank three](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18612)
*Xiutao Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对三比特受控酉门的施密特秩三进行了分类，给出了充要条件，构造了显式示例，分析了其纠缠生成能力，展示了从完全可分态到最大GHZ类态的纠缠输出，并提供了达到最小CNOT门数量的电路分解方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立三比特受控酉门的施密特秩三分类的充要条件，并探究其纠缠生成能力与资源高效电路合成之间的关系。

Method: 利用施密特秩分类理论，构造参数化门族，分析输出态的纠缠结构（包括二分纠缠度量和W-class态的双分纠缠特性），并通过CNOT门计数优化实现电路分解。

Result: 给出了施密特秩三的充要条件；展示了可生成从完全可分态到最大GHZ类态的输出态；构造了参数化门族，其输出W-class态的双分纠缠（AB和AC子系统的二分纠缠）可用单一相位参数的三角函数调控；实现了仅用三个CNOT门的门电路，表明了下界可达性；提供了多种情况下的最小CNOT分解。

Conclusion: 该工作将施密特秩分类、纠缠结构分析与资源高效的电路合成联系起来，为量子纠缠资源的理解和量子电路优化提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: We characterize three-qubit controlled unitary gates of Schmidt rank three, establishing necessary and sufficient conditions for such gates to have Schmidt rank three. We construct explicit examples and analyze their entanglement capabilities, showing that gates with Schmidt rank three can generate output states ranging from fully separable to maximal GHZ-class states. Within this classification, we present a parameterized gate family that produces an output W-class state whose bipartite tangles for subsystems AB and AC are modulated as simple trigonometric functions of a single phase parameter. The examples also include gates implementable with only three CNOT gates, showing that this lower bound is achievable. Decompositions that achieve the minimum possible CNOT count for several other cases are provided as well. Our results bridge Schmidt rank classification, entanglement structure, and resource-efficient circuit synthesis.

</details>


### [144] [Coherence measures in the strictly incoherent operation framework and its application in the multi-path interferometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18631)
*Peiru Li,Jingyan Liu,Ming-Jing Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在严格非相干操作框架下，基于保真度提出了一种新的量子相干度量，给出了解析表达式，证明了其在非相干操作下的单调性，并应用于多路径干涉仪中波粒二象性的刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化相干性是量子基础和量子技术中的重要任务，现有相干度量仍需进一步丰富和完善，特别是在对角态严格非相干操作框架下的度量研究。

Method: 基于保真度提出相干度量，推导其解析表达式，建立与其他相干度量的关系，并证明其在非相干操作下的单调性。

Result: 得到了基于保真度的相干度量的解析表达式，验证了单调性，并给出了若干波粒二象性关系。

Conclusion: 该工作深化了对角态在刻画量子态中的作用，并促进了多路径干涉仪中波粒行为定量描述的进展。

Abstract: Quantifying coherence is an essential endeavor in both quantum foundations and quantum technologies. In this paper, we study the coherence measures in terms of the diagonal states in the strictly incoherent operations framework. Specifically, we propose a coherence measure in terms of fidelity and provide its analytical expression. The relations between the proposed coherence measure and some other coherence measures are derived. Furthermore, we prove its monotonicity under incoherent operations. As an application, we explore the role of the proposed coherence measure in characterizing the waveness in the multi-path interferometer.
