As a result, some wave-particle dualities in terms of fidelity are presented. This work not only deepens the interpretation of the diagonal states on characterizing quantum states, but also promotes the quantitative description of the wave-particle behaviors in the multi-path interferometer.

</details>


### [145] [Memory-assisted advantage for state transfer in disordered quantum many-body scar system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18720)
*Paranjoy Chaki,Ujjwal Sen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了多体疤痕系统中记忆性无序对量子通信的促进作用，发现记忆性无序相比无记忆无序（均匀和高斯）能实现更好的量子态传输保真度，且疤痕程度和非遍历性在其中起到关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究不同类型的无序（特别是记忆性无序）如何影响多体疤痕系统中的量子通信性能，以及疤痕程度和非遍历性在量子态传输中的作用。

Method: 考虑三种无序类型：记忆性无序、无记忆均匀无序和无记忆高斯无序；以最大传输保真度和保真度面积作为性能指标；引入疤痕程度（degree of scarness）进行量化分析；进行标度分析以考察短程和长程传输性能；计算逆参与比（IPR）以表征非遍历性。

Result: 记忆性无序的性能优于无记忆无序通道；最大传输保真度随无序强度先抛物线衰减后线性下降；记忆性无序的疤痕程度更高；记忆效应在短距离和长距离量子态传输中均有益；产生最大传输保真度的态在记忆性无序下具有更大的逆参与比。

Conclusion: 记忆性无序通过增强疤痕程度和非遍历性，在多体疤痕系统中显著促进了量子态传输性能，为量子通信中的无序工程提供了新思路。

Abstract: We analyze how memory in disorder facilitates quantum communication in many-body scar systems. We consider three distinct types of disorder, viz., memoryful, and memoryless uniform and Gaussian, and compare their respective performances in facilitating quantum state transfer. Using the maximum transfer fidelity and fidelity area as figures of merit, we find that memoryful disorder yields a better performance than the memoryless disordered channels. Furthermore, the maximum transfer fidelity exhibits an initial parabolic decay with disorder strength, followed by a linear decrease, for all the disorder models considered. We introduce a degree of scarness, and show that it is higher for memoryful disorder in comparison to memoryless disorders, implying a role of scarness in the quantum state transfer protocol. We further perform a scaling analysis, revealing that memory effect in disorder is not only beneficial for short-distance but also long-distance quantum state transfer. Finally, we show that the state yielding the maximum transfer fidelity has larger inverse participation ratio for memoryful disorder in comparison to the other two disorders, highlighting the role of nonergodicity in enhancing state transfer.

</details>


### [146] [Enhancing the teleportation fidelity of a quantum network using purification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18743)
*Soumit Roy,Md Sohel Mondal,Siddhartha Santra,Indranil Chakrabarty*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了不同拓扑结构（规则网络和随机网络）下两种纠缠分发方案（单路径纠缠交换 vs 多路径纠缠纯化）对量子网络资源fulness的影响，利用平均最大隐形传态保真度作为量化指标，证明了基于纯化的方案能显著提升网络隐形传态能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络支持多种长距离纠缠分发方案（从线性中继到多路径纠缠纯化），不同方案对网络的资源fulness（即支持量子通信的能力）影响不同。需要系统分析和比较不同网络拓扑下不同分发方案的性能差异。

Method: 采用最近提出的平均最大隐形传态保真度作为量化指标；在边使用约束和路径排序约束下，提出了估算该指标的算法；对比了单路径纠缠交换与多路径纠缠纯化两种分发方案；覆盖了规则网络和随机网络等多种拓扑。

Result: 平均最大隐形传态保真度指标对纠缠分发协议的选择敏感；基于纯化的方案能显著提升平均隐形传态保真度，从而改善量子网络的整体隐形传态能力。

Conclusion: 网络纯化方案能够大幅提升量子网络的隐形传态性能，这为设计高性能量子通信网络提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Complex quantum networks can support a diverse set of long-range entanglement distribution schemes ranging from linear repeater protocols to multipath entanglement purification strategies. As a result, a network's resourcefulness, that is its ability to facilitate quantum communication, depends on the deployed distribution scheme. In this work, we analyse and compare the resourcefulness of quantum networks across a broad range of network topologies, including both regular and random networks, under two distinct entanglement distribution schemes. The first relies on entanglement swapping along a single path connecting a source-target pair, while the second exploits entanglement purification using multiple paths between the same source and target nodes. The resourcefulness of the network is quantified using a recently described metric [1] that averages over the maximum teleportation fidelity between arbitrary source-target pairs in the network. We present algorithms for estimating this metric under constraints of edge-usage and ordering of paths. Our results not only demonstrate the sensitivity of the average maximum teleportation fidelity metric to the choice of entanglement distribution protocol, but also highlight the significant improvements enabled by network purification schemes. In particular, purification-based approaches can substantially enhance average teleportation fidelity, thereby improving the overall teleportation capability of quantum networks.

</details>


### [147] [Quantum simulation of neutrino oscillations with bosonic encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18755)
*Sandeep Joshi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用超导量子比特平台中的腔体玻色子模式，通过Fock基编码实现中微子振荡的量子模拟，使用SNAP和位移门设计脉冲序列，实验结果与理论预测高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超导量子比特平台中腔体玻色子模式作为量子模拟的替代编码方案，验证其在模拟中微子振荡这一高能物理问题中的可行性。

Method: 采用Fock基编码将中微子味态映射到腔体玻色子模式上，利用辅助量子比特结合选择性数依赖任意相位（SNAP）门和位移门设计实现所需酉操作的脉冲序列，通过脉冲级控制实现高保真度门操作。

Result: 量子模拟得到的二味和三味中微子振荡概率与理论预测值高度一致，验证了腔基玻色子编码方案在量子模拟中的可行性。

Conclusion: 基于腔体玻色子模式的编码方案能够有效实现中微子振荡的量子模拟，为量子模拟提供了传统量子比特编码之外的新途径。

Abstract: Superconducting qubits offer a versatile platform for quantum simulation. In this architecture, quantum information can be encoded in the bosonic modes of a microwave cavity, offering an alternative to conventional qubit-based encoding schemes. These cavity bosonic modes can be manipulated using a single ancillary qubit. In this work, we investigate the quantum simulation of two- and three-flavor neutrino oscillations using Fock-basis encoding of a cavity mode. We design pulse sequences for implementing the required unitary operations through selective number-dependent arbitrary phase (SNAP) and displacement gates. Pulse-level control is employed to realize high-fidelity gate operations on the encoded cavity mode. The resulting neutrino oscillation probabilities obtained from quantum simulation exhibit close agreement with the corresponding theoretical predictions, demonstrating the feasibility of cavity-based bosonic encoding schemes for quantum simulation.

</details>


### [148] [Universal photon blockade via two-photon light-matter interaction at chiral exceptional points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18756)
*Hai-Tao Dong,Meng-Long Song,Si-Yu Zhang,Xue-Ke Song,Liu Ye,Dong Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了微腔中手性奇异点（CEPs）对光子 blockade（PB）效应的调控，发现CEPs能导致两个相反传播方向的回音壁模式在光子统计上呈现显著差异——一个模式表现出强PB效应，另一个则呈现亚泊松或超泊松分布，为构建非互易多路复用量子光源提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子 blockade 效应是量子光学中重要的非经典现象，但非厄米奇异点对其调控尚未被充分探索。

Method: 理论上研究了含有手性奇异点（CEPs）的微腔中实现通用光子 blockade 的机制，分析了两个相反传播方向的回音壁模式的光子统计特性。

Result: CEPs的存在导致两个相反传播方向的模式在光子统计上呈现鲜明对比：一个模式表现出强光子 blockade 效应，另一个模式则呈现亚泊松或超泊松分布。

Conclusion: 该发现为光子 blockade 的高级应用铺平了道路，并为选择性产生单光子和双光子发射提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The photon blockade (PB) effect is a hallmark non-classical phenomenon in quantum optics and finds important applications for building quantum sources, while the control of PB by the non-Hermitian exceptional points remains largely unexplored. In this work, we theoretically investigate universal photon blockade in a microcavity harboring chiral exceptional points (CEPs) for building multiplexing quantum sources with nonreciprocal photon statistics. The results reveal that the presence of the CEPs leads to a stark contrast in the photon statistics of two whispering-gallery modes with opposite propagating directions. That is, one mode exhibits a strong PB effect while the other displays either sub-Poissonian or super-Poissonian distribution. Our findings thus may pave the way for advanced applications of photon blockade, and provide a theoretical foundation for the selective generation of single-photon and two-photon emission

</details>


### [149] [Efficient simulation of noisy entanglement generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18808)
*Lorenzo Brevi,Federico Grasselli,Alessandro Caraceni,Massimiliano Proietti,Massimiliano Dispenza,Enrico Prati*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过推导解析结果并集成到SeQUeNCe模拟器中，实现了量子网络中纠缠生成模拟的可扩展性和真实感提升，计算时间减少60%以上，并能复现实验数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端纠缠分发是量子网络的关键能力，其真实、高效的模拟对于量子网络设计和应用评估至关重要，现有模拟在可扩展性和噪声建模方面存在不足。

Method: 推导了直接返回纠缠生成协议的成功概率、输出态及保真度的解析结果，纳入多种噪声源（不完美模式匹配、暗计数、不完美存储器初始化），并将其集成到升级版SeQUeNCe模拟器中。

Result: 计算时间减少超过60%，能够复现真实实验的平均纠缠生成时间和最终态保真度。

Conclusion: 该工作增强了当前量子网络模拟能力，使其能够扩展到大规模网络，为未来量子互联网铺平道路。

Abstract: End-to-end entanglement distribution is a key capability of upcoming quantum networks, enabling applications like distributed quantum computing, quantum sensor networks, and secure communications. Hence, its realistic and efficient simulation is crucial for quantum network design and for assessing the ability of a network to run certain applications. This work provides tools to scale-up and improve the realism of entanglement generation simulations in quantum networks. This is achieved by deriving analytical results that directly return the success probability, the output state and corresponding fidelity of a selected entanglement generation protocol, while accounting for a variety of noise sources affecting the protocol. These results are then integrated and streamlined in an upgraded version of SeQUeNCe, one of the most popular quantum network simulators. The resulting simulator features increased scalability by reducing computation time by more than 60%, while allowing for a variety of realistic noise sources, including imperfect mode matching, dark counts, and imperfect memory initialization. The simulator is also benchmarked with real experimental data and is capable of replicating the average entanglement generation time and the final state fidelity of a selected experiment. Altogether, the results can enhance current quantum network simulation capabilities towards large-scale networks, paving the way for the future quantum internet.

</details>


### [150] [Field Demonstration of a Multi-User Continuous-Variable Quantum Access Network for Quantum-to-the-Home](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18840)
*Junpeng Zhang,Xu Liu,Qijun Zhang,Yifeng Liang,Yue Yu,Peng Huang,Huasheng Li,Yingming Zhou,Jingyu Yang,Chunchen Li,Yunfan Chen,Cheng Zheng,Ciqing Deng,Tao Wang,Guihua Zeng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对广播连续变量量子接入网络中的链路不对称瓶颈，提出多用户效用模型选择最优共享调制方差，并在1:16商用光纤现场试验中实现了Mbit/s级渐进安全密钥速率，将理论协议与光纤到户现实相连接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子到户(QTTH)的可扩展部署面临链路不对称导致的调制方差全局优化难题，现有方案无法同时兼顾网络效率与用户公平性。

Method: 在下行广播CV-QAN架构中，建立多用户效用模型以平衡效率和公平性，选取最优共享调制方差；结合鲁棒数字信号处理，在商用光纤上进行1:16现场试验。

Result: 在1:16分路的商用光纤网络现场试验中，实现了Mbit/s级别的渐进安全密钥速率。

Conclusion: 该工作桥接了理论CV-QKD协议与FTTH实际部署之间的鸿沟，为未来可扩展的量子接入网架构提供了指导。

Abstract: Realizing scalable Quantum-to-the-Home (QTTH) faces a bottleneck: link asymmetry in broadcast continuous-variable quantum access networks (CV-QANs) hinders the selection of a globally optimal modulation variance. We demonstrate a downstream broadcast CV-QAN connecting a Quantum Line Terminal (QLT) to multiple Quantum Network Units (QNUs) over commercial fiber. Operating within a trusted local network domain, we establish a multi-user utility model to select the optimal shared variance, balancing network efficiency and user fairness. Supported by robust digital signal processing, our 1:16 field trial achieves Mbit/s-level asymptotic secure key rates, bridging theoretical protocols with Fiber-to-the-Home reality and guiding future scalable access architectures.

</details>


### [151] [Sensitive endoscopic diamond magnetometer for non-contact sensing in confined environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18871)
*Johannes Wesseler,Roland Nagy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种直径6mm的微型化金刚石量子磁力计内窥探头，通过多芯光纤束分离激发与收集通道，在非屏蔽环境下实现了91 pT/sqrt(Hz)的磁场灵敏度，并成功用于锂电池充放电过程的电流密度成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决量子磁力计在小型化与高灵敏度之间的固有权衡，特别是内窥探头因荧光收集效率低导致性能下降的问题。

Method: 采用多芯光纤束将激发与收集通道分离至不同纤芯，配合高数值孔径微型物镜耦合金刚石；基于FPGA的后端实现微波控制、锁相检测和实时共振跟踪。

Result: 传感器探头直径6mm，在非屏蔽环境下灵敏度达91 pT/sqrt(Hz)，测量带宽2 kHz；成功对商用锂离子软包电池充放电过程进行磁场成像并重建深度积分电流密度图。

Conclusion: 内窥式金刚石量子磁力计可在保持高灵敏度的同时适应受限、非屏蔽环境下的测量，为电池技术等领域开辟新途径。

Abstract: Transitioning quantum magnetometry from laboratory environments to real-world applications has been limited by a persistent trade-off between sensor miniaturization and magnetic sensitivity. While bulky systems can achieve high sensitivity, endoscopic probes commonly suffer from inefficient fluorescence collection and reduced performance. Here we resolve this trade-off and present a miniaturized diamond quantum magnetometer with a 6 mm diameter endoscopic sensor head, achieving a magnetic-field sensitivity of 91 pT/sqrt(Hz) with a 2 kHz measurement bandwidth in a magnetically unshielded environment. The fluorescence collection bottleneck is overcome by separating excitation and collection into different cores of a fused multi-core fiber bundle, coupled to the diamond through a custom high-numerical-aperture micro-objective. A compact FPGA-based backend performs microwave control, lock-in detection and real-time resonance tracking, enabling robust operation during magnetic-field imaging. To demonstrate the practical utility of the miniaturized sensor, we image the magnetic field of a commercial lithium-ion pouch cell during charge and discharge and reconstruct depth-integrated current-density maps of the current flow. These results show that endoscopic diamond magnetometers can combine high sensitivity with a probe geometry suitable for confined, unshielded measurements, opening new avenues in battery technology and beyond.

</details>


### [152] [Benchmark of Pauli Correlation Encoding for different optimisation problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18914)
*Fernando Alonso,Colomán Samprón,Jacobo Veiga,Mariamo Mussa Juane,Andrés Gómez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Pauli相关编码（PCE）的量子-经典优化框架，能用多项式数量的量子比特表示m个二进制变量。在QOPTLib基准测试的四个经典优化问题上的实验表明，该方法在NISQ和近容错量子时代具有竞争力，部分情况下能达到或超越传统方法的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子技术在组合优化领域的实际应用潜力，特别是开发一种高效的编码方案，使得在量子资源有限的情况下（NISQ时代）仍能有效处理大规模二进制优化问题。

Method: 采用Pauli相关编码（PCE）方案，将m个二进制变量编码到多项式数量的量子比特上，构建量子-经典混合优化框架。通过QOPTLib基准测试中的四个经典优化问题评估性能，并系统分析了编码压缩顺序、问题结构、超参数选择对解质量的影响，以及后处理、基于shot的执行和硬件噪声的作用。

Result: PCE框架在QOPTLib基准测试中取得了具有竞争力的表现，在多个实例上获得了与基准方法相当甚至更优的解。研究揭示了编码压缩顺序和问题结构对性能有显著影响，后处理能有效提升解质量，而shot噪声和硬件噪声会影响期望值估计的精度和优化过程的动态特性。

Conclusion: PCE编码策略作为NISQ和近容错量子时代的一种高效编码方法，在量子优化方面展现出潜力，能够在有限量子资源下有效解决组合优化问题，为实用量子优化提供了可行的技术路线。

Abstract: The continuous progress of quantum technologies has spurred the exploration of their potential applications across diverse fields, particularly in combinatorial optimisation. In this work, we study a quantum-classical optimisation framework based on Pauli Correlation Encoding, an encoding scheme that can represent m binary variables using a polynomial number of qubits. To evaluate the performance of the method, we use four classical optimisation problems against the instances of the QOPTLib benchmark. The study includes an analysis of the impact of the compression order of the encoding scheme, the problem structure, and hyperparameter selection on solution quality, as well as the role of post-processing in improving performance. Additionally, we study the effect of shot-based execution and hardware noise, showing how these factors influence both the accuracy of expected value estimation and the overall dynamics of the optimisation process. The results indicate that the proposed PCE-based framework achieves competitive performance against the benchmark and, in several cases, obtains equivalent or even superior solutions, highlighting its potential as an efficient encoding strategy for quantum optimisation in the NISQ and near fault-tolerant era.

</details>


### [153] [Exceptional-Point-Anchored Variational Quantum Eigensolver for Non-Hermitian Many-Body Phase Diagrams: Bridging Skin-Effect Topology and Entanglement Criticality on NISQ Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18916)
*Akoramurthy B,Surendiran B,Xiaochun Cheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出双正交变分量子本征求解器（B-VQE），这是一种在嘈杂中等规模量子（NISQ）硬件上模拟非厄米多体系统的量子算法。B-VQE采用独立变分电路表示非厄米哈密顿量的左右本征态，优化双正交目标函数，并集成了异常点检测器（EPD）和非厄米量子几何张量（NH-QGT）读出。通过重要性采样缓解策略避免了基于辅助位的后选择开销。在非厄米Hubbard链、非厄米XXZ自旋链和二维非厄米t-J模型上验证，相对能量误差低于5×10^{-3}，能高精度定位异常点并分辨相边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子算法模拟非厄米多体系统时依赖代价高昂的后选择过程，且未设计用于捕获双正交本征态，难以在NISQ硬件上有效运行。

Method: 使用独立变分电路分别表示左、右本征态，优化双正交目标函数；集成异常点检测器（EPD）通过硬件原生的合并度量识别异常点；非厄米量子几何张量（NH-QGT）用于区分态拓扑和带拓扑特征；重要性采样缓解策略消除基于辅助位的后选择需求。

Result: 在非厄米Hubbard链、XXZ自旋链和二维t-J模型上，相对能量误差低于5×10^{-3}，高精度定位异常点，成功分辨局域化、量子疤痕和趋肤效应等相边界。

Conclusion: B-VQE是一种可扩展的NISQ方法论，可用于构建非厄米多体相图并探索开放量子系统中的拓扑和临界现象。

Abstract: We introduce the Biorthogonal Variational Quantum Eigensolver (B-VQE), a quantum algorithm for simulating non-Hermitian many-body systems on noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) hardware. Non-Hermitian quantum matter exhibits exceptional points, parity-time symmetry breaking, and non-Hermitian skin effects, yet existing quantum algorithms often rely on costly post-selection procedures and are not designed to capture biorthogonal eigenstates.
  B-VQE employs independent variational circuits to represent the left and right eigenstates of a non-Hermitian Hamiltonian and optimizes a biorthogonal objective function that directly tracks non-Hermitian phase transitions. The framework incorporates an Exceptional-Point Detector (EPD) that identifies exceptional points through a hardware-native coalescence metric and a Non-Hermitian Quantum Geometric Tensor (NH-QGT) readout that distinguishes state-topological and band-topological signatures in interacting many-body systems. To overcome the exponential overhead associated with conventional non-Hermitian simulation, we develop an importance-sampling mitigation strategy that removes the need for ancilla-based post-selection while retaining polynomial computational scaling.
  We validate the approach on three representative models: a non-Hermitian Hubbard chain, a non-Hermitian XXZ spin chain, and a two-dimensional non-Hermitian (t)-(J) model. B-VQE achieves relative energy errors below (5\times10^{-3}) and locates exceptional points with high accuracy on noise-free simulations while resolving phase boundaries associated with localization, quantum scars, and skin-effect physics. These results establish B-VQE as a scalable NISQ methodology for constructing non-Hermitian many-body phase diagrams and exploring topological and critical phenomena in open quantum systems.

</details>


### [154] [Probing chaos and thermalization through out-of-time-ordered correlators in random field spin chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18982)
*C Jisha,Shivam Mishra,Ravi Prakash*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过研究随机场海森堡自旋-1/2链中的非时间排序关联函数（OTOC）动力学，揭示了可积与混沌系统在OTOC饱和过程、弛豫行为及统计特征上的差异，并利用本征态热化假说解释了OTOC的长时间饱和行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用OTOC作为信息置乱和量子混沌的诊断工具，探索混沌在多体系统动力学中的印记，并区分可积与混沌行为。

Method: 数值模拟随机场海森堡自旋-1/2链中OTOC的动力学，分析其在不同参数（从可积到混沌）下的弛豫行为、饱和特征及统计性质。

Result: 可积系统OTOC以幂律1/t弛豫，混沌系统先以更高阶幂律（1/t^α, α≥1）衰减后进入指数弛豫；长程谱统计（如数方差）在OTOC饱和附近更有效表征量子混沌；弛豫区对随机场实现敏感，初始置乱区则稳健；推导了OTOC长时间饱和值的精确表达式。

Conclusion: OTOC的弛豫特征可区分可积与混沌动力学，长程谱统计是表征近饱和区量子混沌的有效工具，本征态热化假说可解释OTOC的饱和行为。

Abstract: Out-of-time-ordered correlators (OTOCs) have emerged as a diagnostic of information scrambling and quantum chaos in many-body systems. We investigate the imprints of chaos in the dynamics of OTOCs in the Heisenberg spin-$1/2$ chain with random fields. The system is parameterized to exhibit a crossover from integrable to chaotic dynamics. We demonstrate numerically that the approach to saturation of the OTOC can distinguish between integrable and chaotic regimes, with a power-law $(1/t)$ relaxation for integrable systems and a higher-degree power-law decay $(1/t^α; α\ge 1)$ followed by an exponential relaxation for the chaotic regime. We further show that long-range spectral statistics, such as the number variance, are more effective in characterizing quantum chaos in the regime near saturation of OTOC. We also demonstrate that the relaxation and initial scrambling regimes exhibit distinct and universal features, with the former being sensitive and the latter being robust against different realizations of random-fields. The long-time saturation of OTOC also fluctuates with different realizations, and its exact expression is derived through the Eigenstate Thermalization Hypothesis.

</details>


### [155] [Measurement-enabled online quantum processing with amplitude encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18991)
*Giacomo Franceschetto,Pere Mujal,Rodrigo Martínez-Peña*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于振幅编码的量子储层计算在线协议，通过中电路测量与重置实现部分迹动力学，利用间接测量在不中断时序处理的情况下观测储层，在量子硬件上完成了原理验证。该方法保持在线操作、无需输入缓冲，时间复杂度与时间步数成线性关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有振幅编码量子储层计算方案多采用离线或批处理方式，需要输入缓冲且运行时复杂度高，难以在量子硬件上高效实现可扩展的在线处理。

Method: 结合中电路测量与重置操作实现振幅编码所需的部分迹动力学；设计间接测量方案在不中断时序处理的前提下获取储层可观测量；通过直接测量输入量子比特和间接测量记忆量子比特来监测整个系统。

Result: 在量子硬件上实现了原理验证，通过两个标准基准任务评估了性能，证明可以通过直接测量和间接测量同时观测储层动力学并隔离储层内部演化。

Conclusion: 该工作为可扩展的振幅编码量子储层计算硬件实现提供了实用路线，为复杂量子储层的系统性实验研究开辟了道路。

Abstract: We introduce a quantum reservoir computing online protocol that realizes amplitude encoding on quantum hardware. Our scheme combines mid-circuit measurement and reset operations to implement the partial-trace dynamics underlying amplitude encoding, while an indirect measurement scheme provides access to reservoir observables without interrupting temporal processing. In contrast to other approaches, our method preserves online operation, avoids input buffering, and keeps the runtime linear in the number of time steps. We present the theoretical formulation of the protocol and a proof-of-principle implementation on quantum hardware, and we evaluate its performance on two standard benchmark tasks. Our results show that the reservoir dynamics can be monitored through both direct measurements of the input qubits and indirect measurements of the memory qubits, enabling observation of the full system while isolating the internal evolution of the reservoir. This work provides a practical route toward scalable hardware implementations of amplitude-encoded quantum reservoir computing and opens the door to systematic experimental studies of complex quantum reservoirs.

</details>


### [156] [Coherent Microwave Control of Optically Addressable Donor Qubits in ZnO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19016)
*Ethan R. Hansen,Dong-Rong Wu,Yixuan Li,Yaser Silani,Joseph Falson,Yusuke Kozuka,Masashi Kawasaki,Yuan Ping,Kai-Mei C Fu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究在ZnO中实现了植入$^{115}$In施主杂质的相干微波控制，通过光泵浦初始化与读出电子自旋，观测到10条超精细跃迁及核自旋极化，获得了最高$Ω/2π=36.2$ MHz的Rabi振荡频率和13.8 ns的π脉冲时间，但在低磁场下发现相干时间显著短于此前光学研究报道，排除了微波加热和瞬时扩散等简单解释，为ZnO施主量子比特的微波控制奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ZnO中光学可寻址的浅施主杂质结合了高效自旋选择光学跃迁和同位素纯化长自旋相干潜力，是自旋-光子量子技术的理想平台，但此前缺乏除超快光学脉冲小角度旋转之外的相干控制能力，亟需实现大角度相干操控。

Method: 采用谐振光泵浦初始化与读出$^{115}$In施主电子自旋，结合脉冲光探测磁共振（ODMR）技术，解析了核自旋I=9/2对应的10条超精细跃迁，并通过Ramsey、Hahn回波和动态解耦测量表征自旋相干性。

Result: 实现了最大Rabi频率$Ω/2π=36.2±0.7$ MHz，对应π脉冲时间13.8±0.3 ns；观测到光泵浦诱导的核自旋极化；但低磁场下相干时间显著短于此前高磁场光学研究结果，排除了微波加热和瞬时扩散等解释。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了ZnO施主量子比特的微波控制能力，表明在纳秒级非均匀退相的光学可寻址自旋系统中可实现相干微波控制，为研究退相干机制及开发光接口电子-核自旋寄存器提供了平台。

Abstract: Optically addressable shallow donors in ZnO combine efficient spin-selective optical transitions with the potential for long spin coherence in an isotopically purifiable host lattice, making them an attractive platform for spin-photon quantum technologies. A key missing capability, however, has been coherent control beyond the small-angle rotations accessible with ultrafast optical pulses. Here we demonstrate coherent microwave control of implanted $^{115}\mathrm{In}$ donors in ZnO. Resonant optical pumping initializes and reads out the donor electron spin. Pulsed optically-detected magnetic resonance resolves the ten hyperfine transitions associated with the coupled $^{115}\mathrm{In}$ nuclear spin (I = 9/2) and reveals optical-pumping-induced nuclear spin polarization. We observe coherent Rabi oscillations with a maximum Rabi frequency of $Ω/2π= 36.2 \pm 0.7$\;MHz, corresponding to a $π$-pulse time of 13.8$\pm$0.3\;ns, and characterize the spin coherence using Ramsey, Hahn echo and dynamical-decoupling measurements. Unexpectedly, the measured coherence is substantially shorter than reported in previous optical studies of donor spins in ZnO at high magnetic field. Control experiments rule out several simple explanations including microwave heating and instantaneous diffusion from the driven donor ensemble, leaving an open question regarding the origin of decoherence at low magnetic field in microwave-controlled ZnO donors. These results establish microwave control of ZnO donor qubits with resonant optical access to specific donor species. More broadly, they demonstrate that coherent microwave control can be achieved in optically addressable spin systems with nanosecond-scale inhomogeneous dephasing, enabling field-, temperature-, and materials-dependent studies of coherence-limiting mechanisms and the development of optically interfaced electron-nuclear spin registers.

</details>


### [157] [Quantum circuit decomposition of the tangent-fermion Dirac operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19020)
*C. W. J. Beenakker*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出切线费米子离散化方法，通过将狄拉克方程写为广义特征值问题，避免了格点费米子加倍问题，同时保持了局域性和量子算法的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 格点上的狄拉克算子无法同时满足局域性和无费米子加倍，现有非局域无加倍方案在量子计算中LCU表示项数和范数随格点规模增长，损害算法效率。

Method: 将狄拉克方程重构为广义特征值问题，使用局域算子铅笔（operator pencil），每个铅笔成员具有精确LCU表示，项数与格点规模无关，次归一化因子为O(1)。

Result: 切线费米子离散化逃脱了局域性-无加倍的两难困境，提供了高效的块编码原语，项数和次归一化因子均与椭圆算子相当。

Conclusion: 该方法可实现无费米子加倍的狄拉克谱和格林函数的高效量子块编码，为量子算法提供了实用工具。

Abstract: The Dirac operator on a lattice cannot be both local and free of fermion doubling, at least not without breaking fundamental symmetries. Non-local, symmetry-preserving discretizations that avoid doubling have a quantum circuit representation as a linear-combination-of-unitaries (LCU) in which both the number of terms and their norm (the subnormalization factor) grow with the lattice size, compromising the efficiency of a quantum algorithm. We show that the tangent-fermion discretization escapes this obstruction when the Dirac equation is written as a generalized eigenvalue problem with a local operator pencil: Each member of the pencil has an exact LCU, with term count that is independent of lattice size and with subnormalization factor of order unity, on a par with elliptic operators. This provides an efficient block-encoding primitive for Dirac spectra and Green functions without fermion doubling.

</details>


### [158] [Nonequilibrium steady states induced by stochastic mid-circuit measurements and resets on a quantum computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19027)
*Jakob Murauer,Sabine Tornow,Gabriele Perfetto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在超导量子处理器上实验验证了噪声条件复位协议驱动多体量子系统达到非平衡稳态，最多支持7个量子比特，并观察到与模型平衡量子相变相关的交叉行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管随机复位已被提出作为一种驱动量子多体系统达到非平衡稳态的通用协议，但尚缺乏量子硬件上的实验验证。

Method: 首先构建了一个含噪离散时间理论框架，其中酉门与含噪电路中程投影测量及条件复位交替进行；随后在最多7个量子比特的超导量子处理器上对该噪声条件复位理论进行实验演示。

Result: 噪声条件复位的稳态与实验结果定量吻合，并展现出与模型平衡量子相变相关的交叉行为。

Conclusion: 该结果为在含噪量子器件上制备集体稳态以及进一步开发涉及中程测量的量子算法铺平了道路。

Abstract: Stochastic resetting has emerged as a versatile protocol to drive quantum many-body systems to non-equilibrium steady states by interspersing unitary dynamics with measurements and resets at random times. In spite of this, a quantum hardware validation of such non-equilibrium steady states is still missing. Here, we achieve this goal by first formulating a noisy discrete-time theory where unitary gates alternate with noisy mid-circuit projective measurements and conditional resets. This noisy conditional resetting theory is then demonstrated on a superconducting quantum processor for up to $N=7$ qubits. We consider, as a paradigmatic case, the unitary dynamics of the interacting Floquet transverse-field Ising model. The stationary state of the noisy conditional resetting agrees quantitatively with the experiments, and it shows crossover behavior related to the equilibrium quantum phase transition of the model. Our results might thus pave the way for the preparation of collective stationary states on noisy quantum devices and for further developments of quantum algorithms involving mid-circuit measurements.

</details>


### [159] [Contextuality as a Diagnostic of Translation-Symmetry Breaking in Translation-Invariant 1D Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19033)
*Xiao Zeng,Kaiyan Yang,Lingxia Zhang,Zizhu Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在无限一维平移不变量子链中，语境性不等式（如Bell型不等式）的最大量子违反与自发破缺单点平移对称性产生p>1周期基态严格对应；通过精确有限约化，无限链的语境性验证可转化为小型环上的可测试基准，仅需局域能量测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将语境性从少体关联的实用探针提升为具有真正热力学意义的概念，揭示其与多体系统中自发对称性破缺的深层联系，并为量子多体关联提供可实验验证的紧凑基准。

Method: 解析分析代表性二体/三体见证模型；结合平移不变自适应半正定规划（SDP）层级与变分矩阵乘积态（MPS）算法进行数值验证；建立精确有限约化映射，将无限链的语境性见证转化为p点位周期边界条件不等式。

Result: 最大量子违反点严格对应平移对称性自发破缺（p>1周期基态）；经典界标志着对称性破缺点，竞争经典周期性被唯一量子选择周期取代；量子最优处可精确约化为p位有限环上的不等式，经典界与量子界均保持不变，且若干情形下有限不等式是紧的。

Conclusion: 语境性在无限平移不变链中获得了真正的热力学意义，其最大违反与自发对称性破缺等价；通过有限约化，为当前量子硬件（如超导环、离子阱）提供了可直接检验的多体语境性基准，仅需测量局域能量即可验证。

Abstract: Bell- and contextuality-type inequalities have become practical probes of many-body quantum correlations, often involving only few-body correlators and quantities with a direct Hamiltonian interpretation such as an energy density. Here we show that, in infinite one-dimensional translation-invariant chains, contextuality can acquire a genuinely thermodynamic meaning: within the witness families studied, the maximal quantum violation coincides with spontaneous breaking of one-site translation symmetry, producing strictly $p$-periodic ground states with $p>1$. Along natural continuous interpolations between classical-bound and quantum-optimal Hamiltonians, the classical bound marks a symmetry-breaking point where competing classical periodicities are lifted in favor of a unique quantum-selected period. At the quantum optimum, the studied families admit exact finite-size reductions: a translation-invariant contextuality witness induces a $p$-site periodic-boundary-condition inequality with identical classical and quantum bounds (hence no loss under reduction), and in several cases the resulting finite inequalities are tight. This reduction turns an infinite-chain contextuality certification into a compact, hardware-testable benchmark on a small ring, requiring only local energy measurements. We establish the mechanism analytically in representative two- and three-body witness models and corroborate it more broadly using a translation-invariant adaptation of semidefinite-program hierarchies together with variational matrix-product-state algorithms.

</details>


### [160] [Scalable quantum circuit knitting using a weak-coupling approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19035)
*John P. T. Stenger,Daniel Gunlycke,Nikos Chrisochoides*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在受控近似下实现分布式量子计算的方法，将经典成本从指数级降低到多项式级，并在量子近似优化算法电路上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确的分布式量子计算需要指数级的经典信息来重构量子过程，计算成本过高，需要降低经典通信和计算开销。

Method: 通过识别量子过程中存在一个与其他量子比特弱耦合的量子比特，将量子过程进行分区，从而将经典重构成本从指数级降低到多项式级。

Result: 在基于量子近似优化算法（QAOA）的分层电路中成功验证了该方法，证明弱耦合条件下经典成本可以多项式化。

Conclusion: 受控近似下的分布式量子计算可以在保持计算精度的同时大幅降低经典信息成本，具有实用化潜力。

Abstract: We present a method for performing distributed quantum computing with controlled approximations. Exact distributed quantum computing requires exponential classical information to reconstruct the quantum process. However, we show how the classical cost is reduced to polynomial if the quantum procedure can be partitioned between a qubit that is weakly coupled the other qubits. We demonstrate our method for a layered circuit based on the circuits used for the quantum approximate optimization algorithm.

</details>


### [161] [Matrix Product Operators In The Age of Block Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19083)
*Eugene Dumitrescu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种基于矩阵乘积算符（MPO）的块编码编译器，通过将MPO视为压缩的虚拟路径LCU程序，加速了线性组合酉算符（LCU）的哈密顿量模拟。该方法避免了传统方法中$\mathcal{O}(N^K)$的泡利字符串增长，在Heisenberg链等系统中实现了多项式加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LCU哈密顿量模拟中，随着系统规模增大，泡利字符串数目呈$\mathcal{O}(N^K)$指数增长，成为计算瓶颈。作者希望利用张量网络（特别是MPO）作为中间表示，压缩这种表示并加速量子电路编译。

Method: 提出一种块编码编译器，将MPO视为压缩的虚拟路径LCU程序，重新设计条件PREP和SELECT阶段，使其基于父MPO进行编译。利用张量网络作为量子电路的自然语言和有效中间表示，超越了传统的算子分裂乘积公式。

Result: 在Heisenberg链和微扰Heisenberg-adjacent链的实时演化中验证了数值结果，展示了多项式加速。当压缩MPO的键维度和路径归一化保持温和时，避免了$\mathcal{O}(N^K)$的泡利字符串增长。量化了MPO截断误差和键维度预算对编译多项式表示的影响。

Conclusion: 经典预处理（张量网络数据结构）为加速量子算法提供了新途径，MPO作为中间表示能有效压缩LCU程序，多项式加速在实际问题中可行。

Abstract: We develop a block-encoding compiler that speeds up linear combination of unitaries Hamiltonian simulation programs by treating matrix product operators as compressed, virtual-path LCU programs. In showing how these new conditional PREP and SELECT stages are compiled in terms of a parent matrix product operator, we go beyond typical operator splitting product formulas and illustrate how tensor networks are a natural language and valid intermediate representation for quantum circuits. Our results are numerically verified for two important cases, namely, Heisenberg and perturbed Heisenberg-adjacent chain real-time evolution, and highlight polynomial speedups. Specifically, we highlight a polynomial speedup that avoids the $\mathcal{O}(N^K)$ Pauli-string growth when the compressed MPO bond dimension and path normalization remain mild. We quantify how MPO truncation error and bond-dimension budgets affect the compiled polynomial representation. Our algorithms show how classical pre-processing in terms of tensor network data structures opens new avenues to accelerate quantum algorithms.

</details>


### [162] [Quantum Pump Depletion and Multicomponent Schrödinger-Cat-Like States in Doubly Pumped Intraresonance Kerr Microresonators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19085)
*Ranjit Singh,Alexander E. Teretenkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了双泵浦Kerr微谐振器在腔内共振模式下的量子泵浦耗尽与非高斯态产生。通过将泵浦模式量子力学处理而非经典未耗尽近似，揭示了泵浦耗尽、反作用、纠缠产生、正交涨落和Wigner函数负性来自同一多模动力学。对于n=2腔内分支，约化模型产生具有Z₃对称性的多分量薛定谔猫态，其特征包括Winger负性、非泊松统计、泵浦模式正交压缩和大单模施密特数。与完整Kerr动力学的比较表明，未补偿的SPM/XPM引起的相位剪切会抑制干涉条纹和Wigner负性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统处理中常将泵浦视为未耗尽的经典振幅，这忽略了泵浦耗尽、量子反作用和纠缠等关键量子效应。本文旨在发展一种完整的量子力学处理方法，揭示Kerr微谐振器中多模量子动力学如何产生非高斯量子态，特别是探索Zₙ₊₁离散相结构与对称性在非高斯态生成中的作用。

Method: 从Kerr四波混频选择定则出发，区分了有效共振光子转换模型（约化模型）与包含SPM、XPM和FWM的完整Kerr哈密顿量。约化模型通过初始固定相干泵浦相位，保留了离散Zₙ₊₁相结构；完整模型则保留了算符值非线性Kerr相位。对于n=2腔内分支的四模约化初值问题进行了数值求解和分析。

Result: 在相互作用长度达到生成模式布居数⟨n₁⟩最大且泵浦模式布居数⟨n₀⟩强烈耗尽处，产生了类似猫态的Wigner结构。这些态不是标准的偶/奇相干态，而是多分量薛定谔猫态，具有Wigner负性、非泊松统计、泵浦模式正交压缩和大单模施密特数。完整Kerr动力学中未补偿的SPM/XPM相位剪切会抑制干涉条纹和Wigner负性。

Conclusion: 量子耗尽的腔内Kerr动力学是产生对称性组织的非高斯态的有效途径，为在Kerr谐振器中生成各种非经典量子态提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We investigate quantum pump depletion and non-Gaussian state generation in doubly pumped Kerr microresonators operating in the intraresonance regime. The pump modes are treated quantum mechanically rather than as undepleted classical amplitudes, allowing pump depletion, back-action, entanglement generation, quadrature fluctuations, and Wigner-function negativity to emerge from the same multimode dynamics. Starting from the Kerr four-wave-mixing selection rule, we distinguish an effective resonant photon-conversion model from the full Kerr Hamiltonian containing self-phase modulation (SPM), cross-phase modulation (XPM), and four-wave mixing (FWM). The reduced model isolates the photon-conversion network responsible for the discrete $\mathbb{Z}_{n+1}$ phase structure, whereas the full model retains operator-valued nonlinear Kerr phases. For the \(n=2\) intraresonance branch, the four-mode reduced initial-value problem with fixed coherent pump phases has a residual \(\mathbb{Z}_3\) symmetry and generates cat-like Wigner structures near the interaction length at which the generated-mode population \(\langle n_1\rangle\) is maximal and the pump population \(\langle n_0\rangle\) is strongly depleted. The resulting states are not the canonical even or odd coherent states of Dodonov, Malkin, and Man'ko, but multicomponent Schrödinger-cat-like states characterized by Wigner negativity, non-Poissonian statistics, pump-mode quadrature squeezing, and large single-mode Schmidt numbers. Comparison of the reduced and full Kerr dynamics shows that uncompensated SPM/XPM-induced phase shearing suppresses the interference fringes and Wigner negativity responsible for the clearest cat-like signatures. These results identify quantum-depleted intraresonance Kerr dynamics as a route to symmetry-organized non-Gaussian states in Kerr resonators.

</details>


### [163] [Quantum magic is necessary but not sufficient for wormhole-inspired teleportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19180)
*Sudhanva Joshi,Sunil Kumar Mishra*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过稳定子Rényi熵（SRE）量化量子魔法（非稳定子性），研究了虫洞启发隐形传态协议（WITP）在Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev（SYK）模型各阶段中的动力学行为，揭示了魔法积累与传态保真度之间的区域依赖性关系，并发现结构化魔法重新分布而非原始非稳定子性才是成功虫洞穿越的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子魔法（非稳定子性）在虫洞启发隐形传态协议中的动力学行为，特别是魔法积累与传态保真度之间的关系，以及这种关系在不同温度区域下的表现。

Method: 利用稳定子Rényi熵（SRE）量化量子魔法，在SYK模型上追踪完整纯态在SRE的演化过程（经历混沌化、消息插入、左右耦合和右侧提取四个阶段），并对比耦合与非耦合协议、混沌随机二局域模型，以及不同系统尺寸（N_maj=8,10,12）的表现。

Result: 引力（低温）区保真度与魔法同步上升；峰值尺寸（高温）区魔法在传态开始前即饱和至Haar典型值。基线扣除诊断表明双迹耦合先抑制后引导非稳定子资源向传态信号集中，且该通道振幅随逆温度单调递减。与产生近最大魔法但无法传态的混沌随机二局域模型对比，发现结构化魔法重新分布而非原始非稳定子性才是成功虫洞穿越的关键。魔法在保真度峰值处出现瞬态下降，标识传态事件。SRE经Haar典型值归一化后，不同系统尺寸的保真度-魔法轨迹近似坍缩。

Conclusion: 成功隐形传态需要的是结构化的魔法重新分布而非高魔法量本身，且魔法在传态事件中呈现可识别的时域特征（瞬态下降），这些发现对不同系统尺寸具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of Quantum magic, formally known as non-stabilizerness, quantified by the stabilizer Rényi entropy (SRE), across the stages of the wormhole-inspired teleportation protocol (WITP) in the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) model. By tracking the SRE of the full pure state across scrambling, message insertion, left-right coupling, and right-side extraction, we uncover a regime-dependent relationship between magic accumulation and teleportation fidelity. In the gravitational (low temperature) regime, fidelity rises concurrently with magic from early times, whereas in the peaked-size (high temperature) regime, the magic saturates near the Haar-typical value before teleportation onset. A baseline-subtracted diagnostic comparing coupled and uncoupled protocols reveals that the double-trace coupling first suppresses and then channels non-stabilizer resources toward the teleportation signal, with the channel amplitude decreasing monotonically with inverse temperature. Comparison with a chaotic random two-local model, which generates near-maximal magic yet fails to teleport, demonstrates that structured magic redistribution, rather than raw non-stabilizerness, underlies successful wormhole traversal. Moreover, the magic transiently dips at the fidelity peak, marking the teleportation event in the time domain. Our results are robust across the three system sizes studied ($N_{\mathrm{maj}}=8,10,12$), and the fidelity-magic trajectories exhibit an approximate collapse across system sizes when the SRE is normalized by the Haar-typical prediction.

</details>


### [164] [When Isolated Quantum Systems Appear Classical](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19188)
*Thiago R. de Oliveira,Pedro S. Correia,Tiago Debarba,Gabriel Dias Carvalho,Raúl O. Vallejos,Fernando de Melo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明，在孤立量子系统中，内在的平衡化机制（而非环境退相干）足以使纯态在大多数时间在操作上无法与经典混合态区分。研究识别出两条通往操作经典性的路径：所选性质近似与哈密顿量对易，或探测系统的观测量在平衡后无法访问剩余相干性。经典行为不必局限在能量基矢中，即使在平衡态存在大量相干性时也能出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子到经典转变和热平衡的起源是物理学基础中的两个核心问题。传统解释依赖外部环境（退相干和热浴），但近年发现孤立量子系统也能平衡和热化。本文追问：同一种内在平衡机制是否也能解释经典行为的涌现？

Method: 利用封闭系统中平衡化的严格界限（rigorous bounds on equilibration in closed systems），推导出充分条件，使得时间演化的纯态在大部分时间内，在与所选物理性质相关的操作上无法与经典混合态区分。

Result: 识别出两条互补路径：（1）所选性质与系统哈密顿量近似对易；（2）探测系统的观测量在平衡后失去对剩余相干性的访问能力。经典行为不必局限于能量基矢，平衡态中存在大量相干性时仍可涌现经典性。

Conclusion: 在孤立量子系统中，内在平衡化机制本身即可导致操作经典性的涌现，从而建立了热化起源与量子-经典转变这两个基础问题之间的直接联系。

Abstract: The emergence of classical behavior and the origin of thermal equilibrium are two central problems in the foundations of physics. In the standard accounts, both phenomena are typically explained through interactions with an external environment: decoherence suppresses quantum interference, while coupling to a thermal bath drives relaxation toward equilibrium. Over the last decades, however, it has become clear that equilibration and thermalization can arise even in fully isolated quantum systems, in the operational sense that the expectation values of relevant observables remain close to equilibrium values for most of the time. Here, we ask whether the same intrinsic equilibration mechanism can also account for the emergence of classical behavior. Using rigorous bounds on equilibration in closed systems, we derive sufficient conditions under which a time-evolved pure state becomes, for most times, operationally indistinguishable from a classical mixture associated with a chosen physical property. We identify two complementary routes to such operational classicality: either the chosen property almost commutes with the system Hamiltonian or the observables used to probe the system lose access to the remaining coherence after equilibration. Our results show that classical behavior need not be confined to the energy basis and may emerge even when substantial coherence remains present in the equilibrium state. This establishes a direct connection between two foundational questions: the origin of thermalization in isolated quantum systems and the quantum-to-classical transition

</details>


### [165] [Blind Symmetry Matching in Quantum States with Application to Shot-Count Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19196)
*Mitchell A. Thornton*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需预先知道对称性的量子测量优化方法，通过数据驱动的对称性发现来自动选择最优测量基，从而大幅减少量子计算中所需的测量次数（shots）。该方法适用于有限群，支持弱条件（与表示对易）和强条件（限制在单个电荷扇区）两种对称性检测，并在循环群、二面体群和对称群上展示了指数级的shot缩减效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子测量方法在利用对称性减少shots时假设对称性事先已知，但在实际量子计算中对称性往往是未知的。本文旨在移除这一假设，实现从数据中自动发现对称性并利用其优化测量，从而在更广泛的场景下实现shot数量的大幅缩减。

Method: 提出完整的对称性发现与匹配管线：通过对称性测试对候选群进行评分，选出最大的通过群作为测量基。采用受控twirl操作后接SWAP测试的单一量子电路实现两种条件检测——丢弃群寄存器测试弱条件（与表示对易），后选择群寄存器测试强条件（单电荷扇区）。方法适用于任意有限群，给出了无偏选择规则的证明和电荷发现的完整理论。

Result: 在循环群（傅里叶基）上弱匹配使shots减少10倍至数千倍；在二面体群和对称群（Schur-Weyl）上展示了强匹配的效果；强限制到对称（Dicke）子空间实现了指数级缩减；两系统目标的强匹配额外获得子系统大小的缩减因子。种子演示验证了发现环节的净收益。

Conclusion: 盲对称匹配是量子测量中一种实用的原语，特别适用于无法预先写出匹配基的常见场景。该方法不仅用于shot缩减，还可用于对称性认证、守恒电荷扇区识别、对称性破缺错误检测以及压缩或读出基的选择等多种任务。

Abstract: Measuring a quantum computation in a basis adapted to a symmetry it carries reduces the repeated measurements, commonly referred to as ``shots'', needed to read a statistical answer. Detecting the symmetry a quantum state carries has many uses: certifying a claimed symmetry, identifying a conserved-charge sector, flagging symmetry-breaking as an error signature, and selecting a compression or readout basis; shot-count reduction is developed here as one exemplary case. Existing methods assume the symmetry is known in advance; we remove that assumption. When it is unknown, the carried symmetry is discovered from the data by a symmetry test that scores candidate groups, and the largest passing group is exploited as the measurement basis. We state the pipeline precisely, prove the selection rule is unbiased, and charge discovery in full. Two conditions are treated, both detected by the same score with a different projection: a weak condition, commutation with the representation, and a strong condition, confinement to a single charge sector, the distinction drawn in the quantum-reference-frame literature. A single circuit, a controlled twirl followed by a SWAP test, discovers both: discarding the group register tests the weak condition, post-selecting it the strong one. The framework is general over finite groups, with cyclic (Fourier), dihedral, and symmetric-group (Schur-Weyl) examples; strong confinement to the symmetric, or Dicke, subspace is an exponential reduction. Seeded demonstrations show the loop wins net of discovery: weak matching on momentum readout reduces shots by a factor widening from ten to several thousand, and strong matching on a two-system target by a further factor of the subsystem size. Blind symmetry matching is a practical primitive for the common case where the matched basis cannot be written down in advance.

</details>


### [166] [Spontaneous parametric down-conversion pumped by spatiotemporal structured light](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19219)
*Lukas Montenegro,Rafael F. Barros*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了自发参量下转换中光谱关联的全光学控制方法，通过将泵浦场结构化为空间-光谱波包，利用Gouy相位匹配技术，在标准周期极化晶体中实现了光谱非关联和高维光谱纠缠光子对的产生，其效果媲美非周期极化晶体，并能生成更广泛的量子态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现自发参量下转换(SPDC)中双光子光谱关联的灵活控制，探索无需非周期极化晶体的全光学调控方案。

Method: 提出Gouy相位匹配方法：将光子对投影到高阶空间模式，使泵浦场的空间结构定义非线性相互作用的相位匹配函数；通过同时调控泵浦场的空间和光谱结构（即时空波包），实现对双光子光谱关联的完全控制。

Result: 在标准周期极化晶体中成功产生光谱非关联和高维光谱纠缠光子对，效果与非周期极化晶体相当；当泵浦场为时空关联波包时，可生成更广泛的量子态类别。

Conclusion: Gouy相位匹配方法为SPDC光谱关联控制提供了一种全光学替代方案，避免了使用非周期极化晶体的需求，并拓展了可制备的量子态类型，在量子信息处理中具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Here we investigate the all-optical control of spectral correlations in spontaneous parametric down-conversion. We show that when photon pairs are projected onto high-order spatial modes, the spatial structure of the pump field defines the phase-matching function of the nonlinear interaction. Thus, by structuring the pump field in both space and spectrum, the biphoton spectral correlations are fully controlled.Considering a standard periodically-poled crystal as the nonlinear medium, we show that the Gouy phase matching method proposed here can generate both spectrally uncorrelated and high-dimensional spectrally entangled photon pairs, similarly to what is achieved with aperiodically-poled crystals. Furthermore, we show that our method can generate a wider class of quantum states if the pump field is a spatiotemporal wavepacket, that is, if its spatial and spectral structures are correlated.

</details>


### [167] [Quantum-Classical Auxiliary-Field Quantum Monte Carlo at the Edge of Practicability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19239)
*Francesco Nappi,Matthew Kiser,Fedor Šimkovic*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了量子-经典辅助场量子蒙特卡洛（QC-AFQMC）的算法改进，将主导每步经典计算复杂度从Õ(N^5.5)降低到Õ(N^4.5)。通过应用Aitken块变换处理奇异性Pfaffian、利用算法微分计算力偏置，对100分子轨道体系实现了248倍预估加速。在IQM Emerald量子处理器上对H8进行了含张量网络误差缓解的基态能量计算，并在H12氢链和Li2O4锂超氧化物二聚体体系上验证了方法可扩展性，展示了该方法在早期容错量子计算时代的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QC-AFQMC方法在经典计算复杂度上存在瓶颈，每步计算复杂度高达Õ(N^5.5)，限制了其在化学相关体系的应用规模。本文旨在通过算法改进降低这一复杂度，使QC-AFQMC更接近实际化学问题的处理能力。

Method: 提出两项核心改进：(1) 应用Aitken块变换处理量子试验态与经典Slater行列式walker重叠估计中出现的奇异Pfaffian；(2) 采用算法微分计算力偏置。这些改进将主导每步经典计算复杂度从Õ(N^5.5)降至Õ(N^4.5)。

Result: 对100分子轨道体系实现248倍预估运行时间提升；在IQM Emerald量子处理器上完成H8基态能量计算（结合张量网络误差缓解）；通过无噪声模拟验证了H12氢链的可扩展性；在Li2O4体系（26电子，20轨道）活性空间上验证了方法的实际化学应用能力。

Conclusion: QC-AFQMC在早期容错量子计算时代具有潜力，本文的算法改进使其向处理化学相关体系迈进一步。量子与经典运行时间的估算表明该方法在容错量子计算场景下可行。

Abstract: We introduce algorithmic improvements to quantum-classical auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo (QC-AFQMC) that reduce the dominant per-step classical scaling from $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(N^{5.5})$ to $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(N^{4.5})$ as a function of the number of molecular spin-orbitals $N$. Central to this improvement is the application of Aitken's block transformation to handle singular Pfaffians arising in the estimation of overlaps between a quantum trial state and classical Slater-determinant walkers. Together with the use of algorithmic differentiation for the computation of the force bias, this yields a $248\times$ estimated runtime improvement for a system of 100 molecular orbitals. Using our workflow, we demonstrate a ground-state energy calculation for $H_8$ from quantum data collected on IQM Emerald and post-processed with a tensor-network-based error-mitigation technique. We further validate the method's scalability through noiseless simulation of hydrogen chains up to $H_{12}$, and on the lithium-air battery related rearrangement pathway of the $Li_2O_4$ lithium superoxide dimer in a (26e, 20o) active space. We estimate both quantum and classical runtimes for a potential fault-tolerant implementation of QC-AFQMC, showing that the method holds promise for the early fault-tolerant era. These results move QC-AFQMC a step closer to treating chemically relevant systems.

</details>


### [168] [Random-matrix reduction in projective quantum mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19272)
*Alexey A. Kryukov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个基于投影量子态空间的态空间几何框架，通过一个动力学猜想统一描述了测量、经典性及量子悖论。经典构型空间和相空间作为投影量子态空间的子流形出现，其上Fubini-Study几何诱导出欧几里得经典几何，薛定谔演化的切向分量再现牛顿动力学。测量与环境的相互作用由高斯幺正系综随机矩阵动力学描述，产生各向同性扩散，在微观测量中给出玻恩规则跃迁概率，在宏观系统中稳定经典行为。该随机矩阵猜想并非特设假设，而是源于经典子流形上齐次各向同性布朗运动的酉提升的唯一性。该框架为测量和量子-经典过渡提供了幺正描述，并有望动力学地解决标准量子悖论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子力学中的测量问题和量子-经典过渡长期缺乏统一的几何框架。现有处理方式往往依赖波包坍缩等特设假设，或诉诸多世界解释等非幺正方案。作者希望基于纯几何与动力学假设，构建一个既能自然导出玻恩规则、又能解释经典极限的幺正理论，从而消除量子悖论。

Method: 提出一个动力学猜想：测量设备与环境对量子系统的相互作用由投影态空间上的随机矩阵动力学描述，矩阵从高斯幺正系综（GUE）中抽取。证明该动力学产生各向同性扩散，微观上给出玻恩规则跃迁概率，宏观上稳定经典行为。进一步论证，在经典子流形上自然平移不变性假设下，齐次各向同性布朗运动的酉提升唯一地对应GUE随机矩阵（至多相差尺度与无关标量部分）。

Result: 1) 经典构型空间和相空间作为投影量子态空间的子流形，Fubini-Study几何在其上诱导出欧几里得几何。2) 薛定谔演化的切向分量在经典子流形上还原牛顿动力学。3) GUE随机矩阵动力学产生各向同性扩散，微观测量中给出玻恩规则概率，宏观系统中抑制量子相干性从而稳定经典行为。4) 该随机矩阵猜想在平移不变性假设下具有唯一性。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一个幺正的测量和量子-经典过渡的描述，不依赖波包坍缩等非幺正假设。如果该动力学猜想被接受，则能够动力学地解决标准量子悖论（如薛定谔的猫、Wigner朋友等）。

Abstract: We develop a state-space geometric framework for measurement, classicality, and quantum paradoxes, based on one dynamical conjecture. Classical configuration space and classical phase space for a mechanical system arise as distinguished submanifolds of projective quantum state space. On these submanifolds, the Fubini--Study geometry induces Euclidean classical geometry, and the tangent component of Schrödinger evolution reproduces Newtonian dynamics. Within this framework, interactions with measuring devices and environments are described by random-matrix dynamics on projective state space, generated by matrices drawn from the Gaussian Unitary Ensemble. We show that this random-matrix dynamics yields isotropic diffusion, giving Born-rule transition probabilities in microscopic measurements and stabilizing classical behavior in macroscopic systems. We further argue that the random-matrix conjecture is not an independent ad hoc assumption: under natural translation-invariance assumptions on the distribution of state-space steps originating on the classical submanifold, the unitary lift of homogeneous and isotropic Brownian motion on that submanifold is uniquely given by the Gaussian Unitary Ensemble, up to scale and an irrelevant scalar part. The resulting framework provides a unitary account of measurement and the quantum-to-classical transition and, if accepted, offers a dynamical resolution of standard quantum paradoxes.

</details>


### [169] [Random-matrix reduction in projective quantum mechanics: Numerical simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19273)
*Alexey A. Kryukov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过数值模拟验证了随机矩阵态约化框架，展示了GUE哈密顿量在投影态空间中的各向同性扩散、经典子流形上的布朗运动、Born规则频率以及宏观系统的随机牛顿运动，并比较了GUE与GOE的差异。进一步模拟了双缝实验中的有限分辨率探测记录、Zeno稳定性、高维态空间动力学导致的不可逆性以及张量积粒子-设备动力学。结果表明微观态约化、稳定测量记录、有效不可逆性和宏观经典性可视为同一随机幺正机制的不同粗粒化表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过数值模拟验证理论论文中提出的随机矩阵态约化框架，检验该模型的主要特征预测，并比较GUE与GOE随机哈密顿量的表现差异。

Method: 采用数值模拟方法，对GUE哈密顿量生成的投影态空间各向同性扩散、经典子流形上的布朗运动、Born规则频率、宏观系统的随机牛顿运动进行模拟验证；对比GUE与GOE随机哈密顿量的表现；模拟双缝实验有限分辨率探测记录、Zeno稳定性、高维态空间动力学不可逆性以及张量积粒子-设备动力学。

Result: GUE哈密顿量成功产生所需的各向同性复投影扩散，而GOE无法实现；有限分辨率探测记录、Zeno稳定性、有效不可逆性和宏观经典性均得到验证；微观态约化、稳定测量记录、有效不可逆性和宏观经典性可视为同一随机幺正机制的不同粗粒化表现。

Conclusion: 微观态约化、稳定测量记录、有效不可逆性和宏观经典性可被描述为同一随机幺正机制在不同粗粒化尺度下的表现。

Abstract: We present numerical simulations supporting the random-matrix state-reduction framework developed in the companion theoretical paper. The simulations test the main derived features of the model: isotropic diffusion generated by Gaussian Unitary Ensemble Hamiltonians in projective state space, the restriction of this diffusion to Brownian motion on the classical submanifold, Born-rule frequencies for detector-defined outcome classes, and stroboscopic Newtonian motion for macroscopic systems under repeated environmental monitoring. We also compare GUE and GOE random Hamiltonians and show that GOE fails to produce the required isotropic complex projective diffusion. Further simulations examine finite-resolution detector records in the double-slit experiment, Zeno stability of recorded equivalence classes, effective irreversibility from high-dimensional state-space dynamics and loss of path information, and tensor-product particle-device dynamics in the device limit. The results show that microscopic state reduction, stable measurement records, effective irreversibility, and macroscopic classicality can be described as different coarse-grained manifestations of the same stochastic unitary mechanism.

</details>


### [170] [Floquet framework for driven polar quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19330)
*Viktor Novičenko,Piotr Gładysz,Karolina Słowik,Egidijus Anisimovas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出解析与数值两种Floquet方法处理驱动极性两能级量子系统，推导出包含纵向耦合非微扰处理的 dressed-frame 有效哈密顿量，并建立适用于宽参数范围的数值流方程框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决驱动极性两能级系统中同时存在纵向和横向耦合时，传统近共振Rabi模型及Bloch-Siegert位移无法完整描述系统动力学的问题。

Method: 解析上采用Floquet理论结合dressed-frame变换，对纵向耦合做非微扰处理，在1/ω阶导出有效哈密顿量；数值上建立流方程框架，在宽参数范围内生成时间无关的有效哈密顿量。

Result: 给出了有效横向耦合强度和有效失谐的封闭表达式，并证明在非极性极限下恢复标准Rabi耦合和Bloch-Siegert位移；数值流方程框架在广泛耦合强度范围内均有效。

Conclusion: 该双框架方法适用于驱动极性量子系统、光晶格、超导电路和表面声波固体等多种平台，为强驱动极性量子系统的动力学研究提供了系统工具。

Abstract: We present an analytical and numerical Floquet treatment of a driven polar two-level quantum system characterized by both longitudinal and transverse coupling to a periodic field. Analytically, we derive a dressed-frame effective Hamiltonian up to first order in the inverse driving frequency, incorporating the longitudinal coupling nonperturbatively. This yields closed expressions for the effective transverse coupling strength and the effective detuning, both of which are modified by the presence of the longitudinal interaction. In the nonpolar limit, these expressions recover the usual near-resonant Rabi coupling and the Bloch-Siegert shift. As a second main result, we develop a numerical flow-equation framework that yields a time-independent effective Hamiltonian across a broad range of transverse and longitudinal coupling strengths. This dual framework is relevant for a variety of platforms, including driven polar quantum systems, optical lattices, superconducting circuits, and solids subject to surface acoustic waves.

</details>


### [171] [Quantum solitons and their quantum walks in transmon arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19339)
*Ben Blain,Giampiero Marchegiani,Luigi Amico,Gianluigi Catelani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从理论上研究了由电容耦合transmon构成的线性阵列，该系统可用具有吸引相互作用的Bose-Hubbard模型描述。研究揭示了最低能带中的离散孤子特性，识别出空间局域的量子孤子，并通过量子行走干涉图案展示了其时间演化中的复合孤子性质，最后提出了与当前可调transmon电路兼容的制备方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用超导量子比特（transmon）的人工原子特性和电路可设计性，构建一个可模拟Bose-Hubbard模型吸引相互作用的量子模拟平台，进而研究量子孤子物理。

Method: 理论分析电容耦合transmon线性阵列，将其映射为具有吸引相互作用的Bose-Hubbard模型；通过能谱分析识别最低能带的离散孤子态，并利用时间演化中的量子干涉（量子行走）揭示其孤子复合性质。

Result: 识别出空间局域的量子孤子态，其时间演化展现出量子干涉图案（量子行走），证实了孤子的复合性质；提出了与当前可调transmon电路兼容的实验制备协议。

Conclusion: 超导电路为研究量子孤子物理提供了一个有前景且实验上可及的平台。

Abstract: Superconducting qubits are artificial atoms whose spectra and interactions can be engineered through appropriate circuit design, a versatility that can be exploited for quantum simulation. We theoretically investigate a linear array of capacitively coupled transmons, effectively described by a Bose-Hubbard Hamiltonian with attractive interaction. We revisit the discrete-soliton nature of the lowest-energy band of the spectrum, and identify spatially localized quantum solitons. The solitonic character of these states is revealed through their time evolution, which displays a quantum interference pattern, or quantum walk, highlighting their composite nature. We discuss protocols for preparing spatially localized quantum solitons that are compatible with current state-of-the-art tunable-transmon circuits. Our results demonstrate that superconducting circuits provide a promising and experimentally accessible platform for the investigation of quantum soliton physics.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [Orbital evolution of asymmetric binaries within accreting environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18341)
*Albert Radulea,Marcelo E. Rubio,Konstantinos Kritos,Andrea Maselli*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个研究吸积盘中致密天体极端质量比旋近轨道演化的框架，揭示了盘诱导耗散驱动轨道先快速与盘面对齐、后缓慢阻尼偏心率的双阶段演化过程，并证明相对论效应即使在远轨道处也会产生累积偏差，同时吸积盘模型的选择对演化速率有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端质量比旋近嵌入吸积盘为研究相对论轨道动力学与环境效应的相互作用提供了天然舞台，但两者耦合的长时标演化尚不清楚。

Method: 采用Kerr测地线建模轨道运动，通过有效描述（质量吸积和动力学摩擦）编码盘相互作用，并系统比较了Sirko-Goodman与Novikov-Thorne两种吸积盘模型以及相对论与开普勒近似处理的差异。

Result: 盘诱导耗散驱动双阶段演化（快速轨道面对齐→慢速偏心率阻尼）；相对论效应在远离中心轨道处产生累积偏差；相对论盘结构（更低密度、更大标高）导致更弱耗散和更慢演化；中心黑洞自旋对圆化效率影响较小。

Conclusion: 研究致密天体嵌入AGN盘时，需一致地建模相对论轨道动力学和盘结构，该框架可用于探索长时标演化及与准周期爆发的可能联系。

Abstract: Extreme mass-ratio inspirals embedded in accretion disks provide a natural arena for studying the interplay between relativistic orbital dynamics and environmental effects. In this work, we develop a framework to investigate the secular evolution of compact objects repeatedly crossing an accretion disk around a supermassive black hole. The orbital motion is modeled through Kerr geodesics, while disk interactions are encoded through effective prescriptions for mass accretion and dynamical friction. We find that disk-induced dissipation generically drives a two-stage evolution characterized by rapid alignment of the orbital plane with the disk, followed by slower eccentricity damping. By systematically comparing the dynamics with a purely Keplerian treatment, we show that cumulative relativistic effects produce deviations even at large orbital separations, where the Keplerian approximation would naively be expected to remain accurate. These discrepancies grow through repeated disk crossings and become increasingly pronounced in more relativistic orbital configurations. We further investigate the impact of the accretion-disk model by comparing the Sirko-Goodman and Novikov-Thorne prescriptions. Relativistic disk structures predict systematically lower densities and larger scale heights, leading to weaker orbital dissipation and slower secular evolution. By contrast, the spin of the central black hole has only a minor effect on the overall circularization efficiency. Our results demonstrate the importance of consistently modeling both relativistic orbital dynamics and disk structure when studying compact objects embedded in AGN disks, and provide a framework for exploring their long-term evolution, as well as a possible connection to quasi-periodic eruptions.

</details>


### [173] [A matrix free action of the Ashtekar-Lewandowski volume operator of loop quantum gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18397)
*Waleed Sherif*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了环圈量子引力中AL体积算符的无矩阵作用方法，利用Brunnemann-Thiemann密度表达式和Balakrishnan-Stieltjes表示，通过移位-残差正交（SRQ）和Krylov子空间方法高效计算顶点体积作用，避免了传统密集谱分解的数值瓶颈。实验证明该方法精确保持体积核、快速收敛，并可处理高自旋截断（2j=250000）的大规模数值模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AL体积算符是环圈量子引力哈密顿约束的核心，但其顶点作用传统上依赖有限重耦矩阵的密集谱分解，这严重阻碍了在大运动学Hilbert空间或高价顶点上的数值分析。本文旨在克服这一数值瓶颈。

Method: 利用Brunnemann-Thiemann有向AL体积密度Q_v的矩阵元可通过重耦理论局部生成的性质，结合Balakrishnan-Stieltjes表示将体积近似为(Q_v^2)^{1/4}的移位-残差正交（SRQ）逼近。该方法仅需重复应用Q_v和求解移位正定线性系统，与多移位Krylov方法兼容。理论证明了体积核的精确保持，并给出了算子范数和残差误差估计。

Result: 在嵌入K_5图上的小自旋截断数值验证显示：顶点期望值快速收敛、边界参数依赖可控、零体积模式精确保持。在双倍自旋截断2j=250000下实现了超越密集矩阵化的无矩阵蒙特卡洛估计。结合随机Lanczos正交可在无密集体积矩阵条件下估计固定扇区体积谱测度。

Conclusion: 所提出的SRQ方法有效解决了AL体积算符在大规模数值计算中的谱分解瓶颈，为环圈量子引力中哈密顿约束的高能效数值分析提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The Ashtekar-Lewandowski (AL) volume operator of loop quantum gravity is central to the Hamiltonian constraint, but its vertex action is usually obtained from dense spectral decompositions of finite recoupling matrices, obstructing numerical analysis on large kinematical Hilbert spaces or high-valence vertices. We formulate a matrix free action of the $SU(2)$ AL vertex volume operator in standard recoupling basis, making use of the Brunnemann-Thiemann expression for the oriented AL volume density $Q_{v}$ whose matrix elements can be generated locally from recoupling theory without forming the full matrix. Based on the Balakrishnan-Stieltjes representation of $(Q_{v}^{2})^{1/4}$ we approximate the volume by shifted-resolvent quadrature (SRQ). The resulting action uses only repeated applications of $Q_{v}$ and shifted positive linear solves, making it compatible with multi-shift Krylov methods. We prove exact preservation of the volume kernel, provide operator-norm and residual error estimates, discuss sector-wise scaling bounds, and validate the method on an embedded $K_{5}$ graph at small spin cutoffs against exact dense local-block operators. Numerical simulations show rapid convergence of vertex expectation values, controlled dependence on bound parameters, and exact preservation of zero-volume modes. We further demonstrate matrix free Monte Carlo estimates at doubled-spin cutoff $2j=250000$ beyond dense materialisation, and show that SRQ can be combined with stochastic Lanczos quadrature to estimate fixed-sector volume spectral measures without dense volume matrices.

</details>


### [174] [Implications of Adler-Finch-Skea solution on charged dark energy star satisfying Karmarkar Condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18775)
*Pramit Rej*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在爱因斯坦引力框架下，利用Adler-Finch-Skea解和Karmarkar条件构建与各向异性暗能量耦合的带电奇异星模型（以Her X-1为例），通过Darmois-Israel匹配确定常数，验证了模型无奇点且满足稳定性要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗能量是宇宙加速膨胀的原因，且可与致密天体相互作用。为避免致密天体引力坍缩成奇点，探索将暗能量引入致密星模型是一种可能的途径。

Method: 采用Adler-Finch-Skea解选取时间度规分量，运用Karmarkar条件确定径向度规分量；通过暗能量状态方程（EoS）建立暗能量密度与各向同性完美流体密度的比例关系；利用Darmois-Israel准则进行内外度规的光滑匹配。

Result: 以Her X-1（质量0.85±0.15M⊙，半径8.1−0.41+0.41 km）为对象，构建了带电奇异星模型，分析了度规函数、压强、密度、质量-半径关系及暗能量参数等物理量。

Conclusion: 该模型无奇点，满足所有稳定性要求，是一个稳定且物理上真实的恒星模型。

Abstract: A possible approach for preventing compact astrophysical objects from gravitational collapse into singularities is the idea of dark energy. Since it is the cause of our universe's accelerated expansion, it has the greatest impact on the cosmos. As a result, it appears that dark energy can interact with any compact astrophysical stellar object [Phys. Rev. D 103, 084042 (2021)]. In this study, our primary objective is to develop a simpler model of a charged strange star coupled with anisotropic dark energy admitting the Adler-Finch-Skea solution [J. Math. Phys. 15, 727 (1974); Class. Quantum Grav. 6, 467 (1989)] within Einstein gravity. To develop this model, the Karmarkar condition was employed to determine the radial metric component, while Adler's methodology was used to choose the time-metric component. For this purpose, we explored a particular strange star, Her X-1, with observed values of mass $(0.85 \pm 0.15)M_{\odot}$ and radius $= 8.1_{-0.41}^{+0.41}$ km. In this context, we proceeded to model dark energy using the equation of state (EoS), such that the density of dark energy is proportional to the density of isotropic perfect fluid matter. The unknown constants in the metric were determined by smooth matching using the Darmois-Israel criterion. We conduct an in-depth examination of the stability and force equilibrium of our suggested star framework, as well as several physical characteristics of the model such as the metric function, pressure, density, mass-radius relation, and dark energy parameters. Thus, the physical consistency and stability of the present model are investigated. Therefore, following a comprehensive theoretical investigation, we discovered that our proposed model is singularity free and meets all the stability requirements to be a stable and physically realistic stellar model.

</details>


### [175] [Non-trivial boundary conditions in general-relativistic models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18868)
*Davide Astesiano,Matteo Luca Ruggiero,Federico Re*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出，在广义相对论框架下，暗物质效应可以通过对系统初始条件和边界条件的恰当选择来解释，而不必引入额外物质。以轴对称旋转尘埃流体自引力系统为例，证明了通常归因于暗物质的效应可以通过不同的初始假设重现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战暗物质作为额外物质存在的传统解释，探索在广义相对论框架内通过初始和边界条件的不同选择来替代暗物质效应的可能性。

Method: 从理论分析角度，研究轴对称旋转尘埃流体的自引力系统，通过调整广义相对论方程组的初始条件和边界条件，检验能否在不引入暗物质的情况下重现观测效应。

Result: 通过恰当选择初始和边界条件，轴对称旋转尘埃流体系统能够产生通常被归因于暗物质的效应，表明暗物质可能并非必要假设。

Conclusion: 暗物质效应可能源于对系统初始假设的不同选择，而非实际存在的额外物质；广义相对论本身足以解释相关天文观测现象。

Abstract: We propose an alternative interpretation of dark matter effects within the framework of General Relativity. In particular, we suggest
  that, in astrophysical and cosmological contexts, different initial assumptions about a system inevitably lead to different interpretations of the same phenomena. As a concrete example, we examine self-gravitating systems composed of an axially symmetric rotating dust fluid and show that effects typically attributed to the presence of additional matter, can instead be reproduced through an appropriate choice of initial and boundary conditions for the equations governing the system.

</details>


### [176] [Kiselev black hole and the ultra-slow evaporating behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19110)
*Chen-Hao Wu,Xiao Liang,Ya-Peng Hu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究Kiselev黑洞（浸没在类quintessence暗能量背景中的黑洞）的蒸发行为，发现状态参数$w_q$的降低会降低非最终阶段温度并显著延长蒸发寿命，且其超慢蒸发机制与PFDM黑洞和Horndeski黑洞存在本质差异，为约束$w_q$值提供了潜在途径，并可能补充DESI对thawing暗能量的偏好及超慢蒸发爆炸黑洞的观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过研究Kiselev黑洞的蒸发行为，理解动态暗能量背景对黑洞的影响，并揭示不同超慢蒸发黑洞之间的机制差异。

Method: 通过改变状态参数$w_q$，分析Kiselev黑洞蒸发过程中的温度变化和蒸发寿命，并与PFDM黑洞和Horndeski黑洞进行对比。

Result: 降低状态参数$w_q$会降低非最终阶段温度并显著延长蒸发寿命；Kiselev黑洞的超慢蒸发机制与PFDM黑洞和Horndeski黑洞存在本质差异。

Conclusion: 研究结果揭示了动态暗能量背景对黑洞蒸发的影响，为约束$w_q$值提供了潜在实验途径，并可能补充DESI对thawing暗能量的偏好以及基于超慢蒸发的爆炸黑洞观测。

Abstract: Kiselev solution is a metric that describes black holes immersed in a quintessence-like dark energy background. By introducing a dynamic state parameter $w_q$, the Kiselev solution is supposed to help comprehend the effect of quintessential matter on black holes. In this work, we study the evaporation behaviors of Kiselev black holes. By varying the state parameter $w_q$, we find that the decreasing state parameter lowers the non-final stage temperature and markedly prolongs the evaporation lifetime. We also find that the ultra-slow evaporation mechanism of Kiselev black holes differs vastly from the perfect fluid dark matter (PFDM) black holes and Horndeski black holes, which share the analogous ultra-long lifetime. These results illuminate the effects of dynamic dark energy background on black hole evaporation, provide a potential laboratory to constrain the value of $w_q$, and may complement cosmological and astrophysical observations, e.g., the DESI's preference for thawing dark energy and the observation of exploding black holes based on ultra-slow evaporation.

</details>


### [177] [Impact of the Einstein Telescope's duty cycle on the estimation of binary black holes parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19201)
*Luca Negri,Thomas C. K. Ng,Thibeau Wouters,Tim J. Kuhlbusch,Harsh Narola,Robin Chan,Kailib Ryan Doney,Francesco Cireddu,Isaac C. F. Wong,Fabian Gittins,Peter T. H. Pang,Anuradha Samajdar,Achim Stahl,Justin Janquart,Chris Van Den Broeck,Tjonnie G. F. Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过全贝叶斯参数估计和连续时间马尔可夫链建模的占空比，比较了爱因斯坦望远镜的两种候选设计（ET-Δ三角形布局与ET-2L双L形布局），发现ET-Δ的冗余设计使其多探测器同时运行时间更长，从而在光度距离和源帧质量约束上优于ET-2L，即使信噪比更低时也是如此。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 爱因斯坦望远镜的最终几何构型尚未确定，现有基于Fisher信息矩阵的对比在占空比导致的单探测器运行模式下可靠性不足，需要更精确的处理方法来重新评估两种设计方案（ET-Δ与ET-2L）的科学表现。

Method: 采用全贝叶斯参数估计对双黑洞并合引力波信号进行分析，结合连续时间马尔可夫链对占空比进行精细化建模，并测试不同的探测器维护策略，在模拟的爱因斯坦望远镜上对比ET-Δ与ET-2L的性能。

Result: ET-Δ设计因冗余性可在大部分观测时间维持至少两个探测器运行，而ET-2L常限于单探测器模式；在部分网络运行时ET-Δ优于ET-2L，且多探测器同时运行时间的增加转化为对光度距离和源帧质量更紧的约束，即使ET-Δ中的信噪比更低时仍成立。

Conclusion: ET-Δ设计在占空比影响下的整体科学性能优于ET-2L，冗余布局带来的多探测器协同优势是关键的差异性因素。

Abstract: The geometry of the Einstein Telescope, the proposed next-generation European gravitational-wave observatory, is yet to be finalized. Two competing designs are under consideration: a nested triangular configuration (ET-Δ) and two separated L-shaped detectors (ET-2L). Extensive prior comparisons of ET designs established the scientific landscape using the Fisher-information-matrix formalism and identified that duty-cycle-induced single-detector operation is precisely the regime where this approximation becomes less reliable, underscoring the need for a refined, principled treatment of the duty cycle. In this manuscript, we build on that foundation by revisiting the comparison with full Bayesian parameter estimation of gravitational-wave signals from binary black-hole mergers, projected onto a simulated Einstein Telescope that incorporates a refined duty cycle modelled via continuous-time Markov chains and testing different detector maintenance strategies. We find that the redundancy inherent in the ET-Δ design enables it to maintain at least two operational detectors for the majority of the observing time, whereas the ET-2L configuration is often limited to a single detector. Crucially, we show that, during partial network operation, ET-Δ often outperforms ET-2L, and that the increased multi-detector uptime translates into tighter constraints on the luminosity distance and source-frame component masses. Notably, this remains true even when gravitational-wave events have a lower signal-to-noise ratio in ET-Δ than in ET-2L.

</details>


### [178] [Distinct Near-Horizon Trend of Synchrotron Polarization in Kerr Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19229)
*Yehui Hou,Jiewei Huang,Bin Chen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明，在Kerr时空中，同步辐射线性偏振矢量的近地平线展开具有解析形式。对于稳态、轴对称、退化电磁场，主导阶偏振模式仅取决于Kerr自旋和源极角，而次主导阶修正则编码了电磁场的几何与旋转结构。该结果扩展了先前在赤道面和赤道面外的分析，表明近地平线偏振可作为探测旋转黑洞基本性质及引力-电磁相互作用的潜在探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注赤道面或主导阶的偏振行为，缺乏对近地平线区域偏振矢量完整解析形式的理解，尤其是次主导阶如何编码电磁场结构的机制尚不明确。

Method: 在Kerr背景下，对同步辐射线性偏振矢量进行近地平线级数展开，推导出主导阶和次主导阶的解析表达式，并将其与已有赤道面和主导阶结果进行对比验证。

Result: 主导阶偏振模式仅由Kerr自旋参数和源极角决定；次主导阶修正进一步包含了电磁场的几何结构和旋转信息。

Conclusion: 近地平线偏振观测有望成为探测旋转黑洞基本属性及引力-电磁相互作用的新工具。

Abstract: We show that the near-horizon expansion of the linear polarization vector for synchrotron emission in a Kerr background admits a distinct analytic form. For emission from a stationary, axisymmetric, degenerate electromagnetic field, the leading-order polarization pattern depends only on the Kerr spin and the source polar angle, while the next-to-leading-order correction further encodes the geometric and rotational structure of the electromagnetic field. Our result extends the equatorial analysis of [Hou et al. (2024)] and the off-equatorial leading-order result of [Chael et al. (2026)]. Near-horizon polarization thus offers a potential probe of the fundamental properties of rotating black holes and of gravito-electromagnetic interactions.

</details>


### [179] [A Potential Black Hole Mimicker From Non-Minimal Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19291)
*Debanjan Debnath,Rikpratik Sengupta,Kaushik Bhattacharya*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类在曲率-流体耦合引力理论中产生的无视界、正则超紧凑天体。内部流体与里奇标量的非最小耦合产生了类似真空的状态方程，外部为史瓦西时空，两者通过一个壳层连接。内部度规非奇异，壳层具有刚性物质状态方程，近视界紧凑度可模拟黑洞现象。模型自然选择出典型的超紧凑质-半径窗口（质量1.4-2.1 M⊙，半径5-7 km），并预测了与霍金温度截然不同的几何-热力学壳层温度以及质量无关的光度特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种新型无地平线超紧凑天体，克服现有gravastar模型（如Mazur-Mottola模型）的局限性，寻找能自然预测特定质-半径窗口且具有可观测特征的天体模型。

Method: 在曲率-流体耦合引力理论框架下，引入流体变量与里奇标量的非最小耦合，使内部获得真空态方程；在史瓦西外部与内部之间引入连接壳层，通过壳层匹配条件求解度规和状态方程。

Result: 模型自然预测超紧凑天体质-半径窗口为质量1.4-2.1 M⊙、半径5-7 km；壳层在超紧凑极限下具有与霍金温度不同的几何-热力学温度；模型预测质量无关的光度特征。

Conclusion: 该模型提供了一类新型无地平线超紧凑天体候选，具有独特可观测特征（温度、光度），有望在黑洞候选体观测中加以区分和验证。

Abstract: We present a class of horizonless, regular ultra-compact objects arising in a theory of gravity which allows curvature-fluid coupling. The non-minimal interaction between fluid variables and the Ricci scalar generates a vacuum-like equation of state in the interior, while the exterior remains exactly Schwarzschild. The two spacetimes are glued through a shell at the junction. The interior metric is non-singular, the shell acquires a stiff-matter equation of state, and near-horizon compactness can potentially mimic black-hole phenomenology without event horizons. Unlike the Mazur-Mottola gravastar and its variants, the present model naturally selects a typical ultra-compact mass-radius window, with masses in the range $1.4$-$2.1 M_\odot$ and radii in the range 5-7 km. This framework predicts a unique geometric-thermodynamic shell temperature in the ultra-compact limit distinctly different from the Hawking expression and the other unique observational feature of the model is the prediction of mass independent luminosity.

</details>
